### [1] [The Stokes resistance of an arbitrary particle: a classification of hydrodynamic symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23907)
*Clément Moreau*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用有限维O(3)表示空间和特征标公式，系统分析了刚性粒子阻力算子在不同层次（从平移-力层次到二次流动层次）上的流体动力学对称性。研究发现某些点群对称性在流体力学上不可区分（如Brenner螺旋对称）、手性体可呈各向同性响应（Kelvin各向同性螺旋），并揭示了多面体对称性出现的严格顺序：四面体在剪切流中、八面体通过应力矩、二十面体在二次流中。作者还完成了Jeffery-Bretherton-Ishimoto分类，为力和力矩自由运动提供了参数计数及动力学规范型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Kelvin和Larmor以来已知刚体粒子在Stokes流动中的流体力学响应不能忠实反映其几何形状：离散旋转对称体可能与旋转体无法区分（Brenner螺旋对称），手性体也可能表现为各向同性（Kelvin各向同性螺旋）。作者旨在从群论角度系统厘清不同阻力算子层次上哪些点群对称性在流体力学上是可区分的，并给出各不变空间的维数。

Method: 将阻力算子视为有限维O(3)表示空间的元素，利用特征标公式（character formulae）判定每个层次上哪些点群对称性在流体力学上可区分，计算每个不变空间的维数。通过将阻力算子投影到力和力矩自由运动上，得到对称性参数计数并构造相应的动力学规范型。

Result: 得到从平移-力层次到二次流动层次的显式嵌套流体力学对称性群序列；发现几何上无法实现的水动力学类别；赋予螺旋对称性层次依赖的定义；证明多面体对称性按严格顺序可见：四面体对称性在剪切流中、八面体对称性通过应力矩、二十面体对称性在二次流中；完成Jeffery-Bretherton-Ishimoto分类；表征所有产生Jeffery动力学的流体力学类别；识别可产生不规则全姿态动力学的手性四面体和八面体规范型。

Conclusion: 该框架将粒子流体力学响应中的对称性结构完全刻画出来，不仅揭示了形状几何与流体力学可观测性之间的深层关系，还为轴对称情形的Jeffery-Bretherton-Ishimoto理论提供了完整的一般化，展现了手性四面体和八面体粒子可能产生不规则全姿态动力学的可能。

Abstract: The linearity of the Stokes equations organises the hydrodynamic response of a rigid particle into a hierarchy of resistance operators, coupling successive truncations of the ambient-flow jet to moments of the surface traction. Since the work of Kelvin and Larmor, it has been known that this response does not resolve particle geometry faithfully: bodies with discrete rotational symmetry may be indistinguishable from bodies of revolution (Brenner's helicoidal symmetry) and a chiral body may respond isotropically, as in Kelvin's isotropic helicoid. We regard the resistance operators as elements of finite-dimensional O(3)-representation spaces and use character formulae to determine, at every level of the hierarchy, which point-group symmetries are hydrodynamically distinguishable and the dimension of each invariant space. This yields an explicit nested sequence of hydrodynamic symmetry-group sets, from the translation-force level to the quadratic-flow level. The framework reveals hydrodynamic classes that no shape can realise geometrically, gives helicoidal symmetry a level-dependent definition, and shows that polyhedral symmetry becomes visible in a strict order: tetrahedral symmetry in shear, octahedral symmetry through the stresslet, and icosahedral symmetry in quadratic flow. Projecting the resistance operators onto force- and torque-free motion provides symmetry-based parameter counts and a constructive route to the corresponding dynamical normal forms. We thereby complete the Jeffery-Bretherton-Ishimoto classification, characterise all hydrodynamic classes producing Jeffery dynamics, and identify chiral tetrahedral and octahedral normal forms that can generate irregular full-attitude dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Momentum-scalar coupled turbulence with anomalous momentum and scalar diffusions. Part 1: Without external force and with long-range external force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23946)
*Wei Zhao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个动量-标量耦合湍流的理论模型，其中两个场均表现出由分数阶双调和算子（阶数分别为γ/4和α/4）描述的异常扩散。作者推导了动能谱和标量谱的解析表达式，定义了特征波数（Kolmogorov尺度倒数k_K和标量耗散尺度倒数k_S），并引入了异常Schmidt数Sc_Z来表征级联拓扑。该理论在γ=α=2时退化为经典Kolmogorov-Obukhov-Corrsin-Batchelor标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典湍流理论假设正常扩散（γ=α=2），但在复杂湍流系统中，动量与标量输运往往表现出非Fickian（异常扩散）行为。现有理论缺乏统一框架来描述这种动量-标量耦合的异常扩散湍流，因此需要建立更一般的理论模型。

Method: 采用分数阶双调和算子分别描述动量场和标量场的异常扩散（阶数γ/4和α/4），聚焦长程外强迫或无强迫湍流，通过解析推导获得动能谱E_u(k)、标量谱E_s(k)以及特征波数k_K和k_S的表达式，并定义异常Schmidt数Sc_Z = k_0^{γ-α}(c_u/c_s)描述级联拓扑。

Result: 推导出动能谱和标量谱的解析表达式，以及特征波数k_K和k_S关于γ、α、湍流耗散率ε_u、动量扩散率c_u和标量扩散率c_s的函数关系。发现超扩散（γ<2或α<2）会反直觉地增大k_K和k_S，从而拓宽惯性区。当γ=α=2时，理论退化为经典的Kolmogorov-Obukhov-Corrsin-Batchelor标度律。

Conclusion: 该理论统一了经典湍流标度律并推广到异常扩散情形，为理解复杂湍流系统中的非Fickian输运提供了理论基础，异常Schmidt数作为控制级联拓扑的关键参数揭示了动量与标量扩散时间尺度比值在最小波数处的决定性作用。

Abstract: We present a theoretical model for momentum--scalar coupled turbulence in which both fields undergo anomalous diffusion, described by fractional biharmonic operators of orders $γ/4$ and $α/4$, respectively. Focusing on the long-range external forcing or unforced turbulence, we derive analytical expressions for the kinetic energy spectrum $E_u(k)$, the scalar spectrum $E_s(k)$, and the characteristic wavenumbers $k_K = \left( \frac{ε_u^{1/3}}{c_u} \right)^{1/(γ- 2/3)}$ (reciprocal of Kolmogorov scale) and $k_S = \left( \frac{ε_u^{1/3}}{c_s} \right)^{1/(α- 2/3)}$ (reciprocal of scalar dissipation scale) as functions of $γ$, $α$, turbulent dissipation rate $ε_u$, diffusivities of momentum ($c_u$) and scalar ($c_s$), respectively. An anomalous Schmidt number $Sc_Z = k_0^{γ- α} \frac{c_u}{c_s}$ is defined to governs the cascade topology. It describes the ratio of diffusion times of scalar and momentum on the minimum wavenumber $k_0$. Superdiffusion ($γ<2$ or $α<2$) is shown to counter-intuitively enlarge $k_K$ and $k_S$, broadening the inertial range. The theory unifies the classical Kolmogorov--Obukhov--Corrsin--Batchelor scalings as special cases when $γ=α=2$, and provides a foundation for understanding non-Fickian transport in complex turbulent systems.

</details>


### [3] [Momentum-scalar coupled turbulence with anomalous momentum and scalar diffusions. Part 2: With short-range external force and implementation in electrokinetic turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23947)
*Wei Zhao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将广义反常扩散框架扩展到短程力驱动区域（β>2/3），以电动力学（EK）湍流（β=1）为例，推导了Quad-cascade过程四个子区间中的速度与标量耗散波数（k_MD和k_SD），并结合Golestanian反常扩散机制构建了相图，识别出两个新的频谱子区间：对流-黏性子区间和扩散-强迫子区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此研究旨在将先前建立（第一部分）的广义反常扩散框架从远程力驱动区域（β≤2/3）扩展到短程力驱动区域（β>2/3），因为在该区域多尺度力主导（MFD）子区间与耗散区间直接竞争，导致新的级联拓扑结构出现，需要对电动力学湍流进行完整的解析描述。

Method: 基于Quad-cascade过程（惯性、恒定Π_u、恒定Π_s、可变通量子区间），推导速度与标量耗散波数k_MD和k_SD的表达式；将Golestanian关于电解质的反常扩散预测纳入分析，利用尺度依赖的反常Schmidt数Sc_Z构建相图，分析四个子区间中耗散波数的相对大小关系。

Result: 识别出两个仅在短程力驱动区域出现的新频谱子区间：（1）对流-黏性子区间（k_MD ≪ k ≪ k_SD），当Sc_Z ≫ 1时出现，标量场驱动黏性流动，得到E_u ~ k^{-(3/2 + ξ_s/2)}并伴有拉伸指数截断；（2）扩散-强迫子区间（k_SD ≪ k ≪ k_MD），当Sc_Z ≪ 1且γ ≤ α时出现，标量耗散范围决定电强迫，得到E_s ~ k^{ξ_u - 2α}。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了反常扩散条件下EK湍流的完整解析图谱，揭示了电场强度和离子扩散率等外部参数如何决定级联拓扑结构，为理解短程力驱动区域中多尺度力与耗散过程的竞争机制提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We extend the generalized anomalous diffusion framework established in Part I to the short-range forcing regime ($β> 2/3$), where the multiscale-force dominated (MFD) subrange is intercalated after the inertial subrange, competing directly with the dissipation ranges. Focusing on electrokinetic (EK) turbulence as the prototypical example with $β=1$, we derive the relations for the velocity and scalar dissipation wavenumbers, $k_{MD}$ and $k_{SD}$, across all four subranges of the Quad-cascade process (inertial, constant-$Π_u$, constant-$Π_s$, and variable flux). By incorporating Golestanian's predicted anomalous diffusion regimes for electrolytes, we construct comprehensive phase diagrams showing how the relative magnitudes of $k_{MD}$ and $k_{SD}$ are governed by the scale-dependent anomalous Schmidt number $Sc_Z$. We identify two new spectral subranges that emerge exclusively in this short-range forcing regime: (i) the convective-viscous subrange ($k_{MD} \ll k \ll k_{SD}$) of velocity spectrum for $Sc_Z \gg 1$, where the scalar field drives a viscous flow yielding $E_u \sim k^{-\left(\frac{3}{2} + \frac{ξ_s}{2}\right)}$ with a stretched-exponential cutoff; and (ii) the diffusive-forcing subrange ($k_{SD} \ll k \ll k_{MD}$) of scalar spectrum for $Sc_Z \ll 1$ and $γ\leq α$, where the scalar dissipation range determines the electric forcing, leading to $E_s \sim k^{ξ_u - 2α}$. These results provide a complete analytical map of EK turbulence under anomalous diffusion, revealing how external parameters such as electric field strength and ionic diffusivity determine the cascade topology.

</details>


### [4] [Backflow-Induced Inertial Arrest of Velocity Fluctuations in Sedimenting Suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23997)
*Hsien-Hung Wei*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个自洽的流体动力学理论，通过引入回流的惯性筛选机制解决了沉积悬浮液中Stokes速度涨落发散与实验有限测量值之间的矛盾。理论预测筛选长度ξ~aφ^{-1/3}Re_p^{-1/3}远大于平均粒子间距，并推导出速度涨落δu~φ^{1/3}V_sRe_p^{-1/6}、相关时间τ_c=ξ²/ν以及流体动力学自扩散系数D_H~V_s a等标度律，预测的普适常数与实验定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉积悬浮液中，纯粹的Stokes流体动力学理论预测速度涨落会随时间呈发散性增长（长期尾效应导致），但实验测量显示速度涨落是有限的。这一理论与实验之间的巨大分歧长期未获解决，论文旨在建立统一的理论框架来调和二者。

Method: 论文提出自洽的流体动力学理论：关键创新在于认识到补偿回流（backflow）即使在极小的粒子Reynolds数下也会产生不可忽略的惯性效应，形成特征筛选长度ξ~aφ^{-1/3}Re_p^{-1/3}。该惯性筛选与有限时间粘性扩散相结合，截断了大规模速度涨落的不限时空增长。理论无需可调参数即可推导各标度律的普适常数。

Result: 理论预测速度涨落标度δu~φ^{1/3}V_sRe_p^{-1/6}，粘性相关时间τ_c=ξ²/ν，并成功复现了已知的流体动力学自扩散系数标度D_H~V_s a。无参数预测与实验定量吻合，并成功捕捉了从有限相关区间向有限系统区间的实验交叉行为。

Conclusion: 该工作表明回流惯性筛选是沉积悬浮液速度涨落的关键物理机制，为长期悬而未决的Stokesian速度涨落发散问题提供了自洽的解析解，并统一解释了实验观测到的多个标度行为。

Abstract: A self-contained hydrodynamic theory is proposed to reconcile the discrepancy between divergent Stokesian velocity fluctuations and finite experimental measurements in sedimenting suspensions. We show that the compensating backflow induces non-negligible inertia, giving rise to an emergent screening length $ξ\sim aφ^{-1/3}Re_p^{-1/3}$ far exceeding the mean interparticle spacing $aφ^{-1/3}$ even at vanishingly small particle Reynolds numbers. This backflow inertial screening, together with finite-time viscous diffusion, arrests the indefinite spatiotemporal growth of large-scale velocity fluctuations. The resulting velocity fluctuations scale as $δu \sim φ^{1/3}V_sRe_p^{-1/6}$, together with the viscous correlation time $τ_c=ξ^2/ν$, reproducing the well-known hydrodynamic self-diffusivity scaling $D_H\sim V_s a$. The theory predicts the prefactors of these scaling laws without adjustable parameters, in good quantitative agreement with experimental measurements. It also successfully captures the experimentally observed crossover from the finite-correlation regime to the finite-system regime as the screening length becomes comparable to the system size.

</details>


### [5] [Assessment of turbulent fire dynamics and combustion instabilities using a flamelet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24323)
*Yunxiao Yan,Fabian Brännström,Christian Hasse,Xu Wen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究使用基于flamelet/progress variable (FPV)方法的大涡模拟(LES)对Sandia甲烷火羽流基部附近的燃烧不稳定性进行了数值研究，考虑了有限速率化学和微分扩散效应，并与EDM模型及实验数据对比验证了模型性能，通过涡量方程预算分析阐明了火羽底部蜂窝状流动结构的形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sandia一立方米甲烷火羽流是大型燃烧建模的经典基准，但靠近火羽流基部的燃烧不稳定性尚未被充分理解。需要评估FPV方法在大型火灾羽流中的预测性能，并阐明近火底区域燃烧不稳定性的物理机理。

Method: 采用flamelet/progress variable (FPV)方法结合有限速率化学和微分扩散，通过LES模拟大型火羽流；系统研究了辐射模型和网格分辨率的影响，将轴向和径向速度与实验数据对比；利用涡量方程的预算分析研究近火底蜂窝结构流动的形成机制。

Result: 所有模型均能捕捉火羽流的主要流动特征，但包含微分扩散的FPV模型在近火羽流底座区域的预测精度更高。通过涡量方程预算分析明确了控制蜂窝结构形成的失稳类型，并量化了有限速率化学和微分扩散对热化学量预测的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了湍流火羽流基部燃烧不稳定性的底层物理机制，为flamelet模型在气池火灾LES中的性能提供了新见解，并为大规模湍流浮力驱动火焰的高保真数值模拟提供了可靠指导。

Abstract: The Sandia one-meter methane fire plume is an established benchmark for turbulent combustion modeling of large-scale flames. This study investigates the combustion instabilities formed close to the base of the Sandia fire plume. Finite rate chemistry and differential diffusion are considered using a flamelet/progress variable (FPV) approach. The performance of the FPV approach is assessed by comparing with the eddy dissipation model (EDM) and the experimental data for the large-scale fire plume via large eddy simulations (LES). The effects of radiation modeling and mesh resolution on the predictive capability of the model are systematically investigated by comparing the axial and radical velocities against the experimental data at various locations. Although all models successfully capture the primary flow characteristics of fire plumes, the FPV model with differential diffusion yields improved predictions in the near-flame-base region. The formation mechanism of cellular flow structures near the flame base is investigated via a budget analysis of the vorticity equation, and the type of instability governing the formation of the cellular structure is clarified. Finally, the individual effects of finite rate chemistry and differential diffusion on the prediction of the thermo-chemical quantities are quantified. Overall, this study explains the underlying physics governing combustion instabilities at the base of turbulent fire plumes, provides novel insights into the performance of flamelet models for LES of gaseous pool fires, and offers reliable guidance for the high-fidelity numerical simulation of large-scale turbulent buoyancy driven flames.

</details>


### [6] [Feedback control of vortex shedding using data-driven modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24435)
*Jack Proudfoot,Chris J. Nicholls,Brian M. T. Tang,Marko Bacic*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了Re=1000下圆柱绕流涡脱落的基于数据驱动的降阶建模与反馈控制。研究发现，对于LQG控制而言，并非阶数越高越好——超过14阶的控制器性能显著下降，而最佳性能仅需9个模态即可实现（实现28.6 dB的升力系数方差降低和26%的阻力减小）。通过Bode积分定理和频域分析表明，性能下降部分归因于经典的"水床效应"，即未建模动态频带内的灵敏度增加。更重要的是，基于2D URANS数据训练的控制模型在3D DDES仿真中仍能实现13.7%的显著阻力降低，证明了2D URANS足以用于降阶模型生成和控制设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆柱绕流涡脱落是流体力学中的经典问题，对其进行主动流动控制具有重要意义。然而，在较高雷诺数（Re=1000）下，高保真控制面临计算成本高、模型复杂等挑战。作者希望探索：(1)降阶模型的阶数对控制性能的影响规律；(2)低维线性模型是否足以设计有效的反馈控制器；(3)基于二维模拟数据训练的控制器能否适用于三维实际流场。

Method: 采用动态模式分解带控制（DMDc）方法，从2D非定常模拟（URANS）的训练数据中提取线性降阶状态空间模型。以升力测量为唯一反馈量，设计不同阶数（从4阶到14阶以上）的LQG控制器，并通过Bode积分定理和频域分析解释控制器性能随阶数变化的机理。控制器性能在2D模拟和3D DDES仿真中分别进行了验证，同时考察了外部扰动、噪声抑制和参数不确定性的影响。

Result: (1)至少需要4阶模型才能实现LQG控制器对涡脱落的抑制，最佳性能出现在9个模态（模数）时；(2)阶数超过14的控制器性能显著下降，这是水床效应在未建模动态频带中增大灵敏度的结果；(3)在2D模拟中，升力系数方差降低28.6 dB，阻力降低26%；(4)尽管仅在2D URANS中训练，该控制器在3D DDES中仍实现13.7%的阻力降低，证明了2D数据训练的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文表明，对于涡脱落抑制的反馈控制，降阶模型存在最优阶数区间，阶数过高反而因水床效应导致鲁棒性下降。此外，2D URANS仿真数据足以用于降阶模型的构建和控制设计，即使在3D实际流动中也能实现显著的减阻效果，这为高雷诺数流动控制提供了一种经济高效的建模与控制设计路径。

Abstract: This paper details the data-driven modelling and feedback control of vortex shedding past a circular cylinder at a Reynolds number of Re = 1000. We study the effect of varying the order of the reduced model for control design purposes and demonstrate that higher orders can lead to lower suppression of vortex shedding. We use the Bode integral theorem and a frequency-domain interpretation to show that this drop in performance is, in part, due to the classical ``waterbed effect'', which increases sensitivity in frequency bands of unmodelled dynamics. Training data from 2D unsteady simulation is used to obtain linear reduced-order state-space models of the system via dynamic mode decomposition with control. Using only lift measurement, we show that at least a 4th-order model is required for an LQG controller to suppress vortex shedding, with the best performance achieved with as few as 9 modes, whilst higher-order (>14) controllers show a significant decrease in performance. We study the influence of external disturbances, noise rejection, and parameter uncertainty on controller performance. A 28.6 dB reduction in lift coefficient variance is achieved, resulting in a 26% reduction in drag. We further show that, for control design purposes with practical actuation bandwidth, the closed-loop control delivers a significant 13.7% drag reduction within 3D DDES, despite having been trained with 2D URANS and therefore argue that 2D URANS simulation is sufficient for reduced-order model generation and control design.

</details>

Abstract: The effects of pressure on the chemical sooting structure of equi-diffusive soot formation (SF) in a counterflow diffusion flame (CDF) are explored in a combined experimental and numerical study over pressures ranging from 1 bar to 6 bar. Experiments preserve the diffusive flame structure and carbon flux at increasing pressures and utilize measurements of soot concentrations, dispersion exponents and soot production rates. Numerical simulations are completed in OpenSMOKE++ with detailed \ce{C1}-\ce{C16} chemistry, lumped PAH consideration up to \ce{C160}, sectional soot model and tracking of the C/H ratio in particulates. Network-based tools are utilized to study the organization and evolution of carbon routing pathways. Results show that for equi-diffusive flames, soot concentration increases with residence time and pressure, whereas soot production rates are influenced only by pressure. Soot C/H ratio is observed to increase with pressure using numerical and experimental methods, but numerical solutions underestimate the increase in hydrogen abstraction reactions. The soot-forming network undergoes a percolation-like organization of its pathways before soot inception. The network then continues to grow by adding connections through its influential nodes. Acetylene is identified as a highly influential node in the carbon transfer graph, with its influence increasing with pressure.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外部振荡流对冰熔化动力学的影响，探索对流与扩散在边界层内相互作用所决定的不同熔化机制，为相关工程与自然现象提供理论依据。

Result: 识别出两种截然不同的熔化机制：慢振荡时熔化过程可用有效稳态流和经典强制对流描述；快振荡时熔化时间显著增加并趋近扩散极限，因为振荡流无法更新振荡边界层内的流体，冷融水积聚在界面处抑制了热传递。

Conclusion: 熔化动力学由边界层内对流与扩散的竞争主导，振荡频率是决定熔化从强制对流控制过渡到扩散控制的关键参数。

Abstract: We investigate the melting dynamics of an ice disk subjected to an external oscillatory flow using two-dimensional direct numerical simulations, in the absence of buoyancy, varying the flow amplitude and its oscillation frequency. We identify two distinct regimes governed by the interplay between advection and diffusion within the boundary layer. For slow oscillations, the melting process is well described by an effective steady flow, where a description based on classical forced convection is applicable. For fast oscillations the melting time increases significantly and approaches the diffusion limit regime, as a result of the oscillatory flow being unable to renew the fluid within the oscillating boundary layer, causing cold meltwater to accumulate near the interface and reducing heat transfer.

</details>

### [9] [Equivariant Cellular Sheaves for Molecular Electronic Structure: Bridging Sheaf Cohomology and E(3)-Equivariant Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23571)
*Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构性的观察：在局域原子轨道基下，分子的单粒子哈密顿量（经过常数平移使其半正定）可视为构建于分子之上的规则胞腔复形的细胞层（cellular sheaf）的拉普拉斯算子。通过将限制映射设为依赖于键几何的O(3)-可转向双中心核函数，该构造恢复了Slater-Koster形式作为特例，并得到一个E(3)与置换等变的算子。作者证明了零阶上同调H^0对应非键（零模）轨道，Hodge 1-拉普拉斯算子使环携带循环与离域信息，并证明该模型严格推广了E(3)等变消息传递网络与CW网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子性质预测主要依赖E(3)等变消息传递网络及等变哈密顿量预测，而拓扑深度学习将图网络推广到细胞层（cellular sheaves）。作者的核心动机是将这两个看似独立的领域统一起来：揭示哈密顿量在局域原子轨道基下本质上是一个细胞层的拉普拉斯算子这一结构等价性，从而为等变哈密顿量预测提供严格的拓扑理论支撑与新的不变量。

Method: 方法的核心步骤：(1) 在局域原子轨道基下，经常数平移使哈密顿量变为半正定后，将其识别为分子规则胞腔复形上的细胞层拉普拉斯算子；(2) 限制映射构造为基于键几何的O(3)-可转向双中心核函数，Slater-Koster形式为其特例，从而得到E(3)与置换等变的算子；(3) 利用H^0 = ker L刻画非键轨道，使用Hodge 1-拉普拉斯算子使环（高阶胞腔）携带循环与离域信息；(4) 证明该等变细胞层网络（Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Network）的等变性、表达力与上同调对应定理。

Result: 数值实验验证：(1) 哈密顿量到细胞层的嵌入达到机器精度；(2) 上同调维数在11个共轭分子上正确复现非键轨道计数；(3) 细胞层拉普拉斯算子的O(3)等变性达到机器精度；(4) 等变模型在定向电子性质目标上取得更低的误差与更好的旋转泛化性能。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于为等变哈密顿量预测提供了细胞层（sheaf-theoretic）形式化框架及其拓扑不变量，而非提出新的哈密顿量预测方法本身。该框架将Slater-Koster理论与拓扑深度学习统一，继承了非平凡细胞层扩散的抗过平滑性质，并为分子拓扑结构（如环与离域）提供了严格的上同调刻画。

Abstract: Equivariant message-passing networks are the standard model for molecular property and interatomic-potential prediction, and recent work predicts the electronic Hamiltonian itself in an E(3)-equivariant way. Separately, topological deep learning has extended graph networks to cellular sheaves. Our central observation is structural: in a localized atomic-orbital basis, the molecular single-particle Hamiltonian, after a constant shift that makes it positive semidefinite, is the Laplacian of a cellular sheaf on a regular cell complex built from the molecule. Making the restriction maps O(3)-steerable two-center kernels from bond geometry recovers the Slater-Koster form as a special case and yields an E(3)- and permutation-equivariant operator. Three consequences follow. First, the zeroth sheaf cohomology H^0 = ker L is a topological invariant equal to the non-bonding (zero-mode) orbitals, recovering the classical alternant non-bonding-orbital count as a lower bound. Second, the Hodge 1-Laplacian lets higher cells (rings) carry cycle and delocalization information through H^1. Third, the model strictly generalizes E(3)-equivariant message-passing networks and CW networks, and inherits the anti-oversmoothing of non-trivial sheaf diffusion. We prove equivariance, expressivity, and cohomological-correspondence results for the Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Networks, and validate them numerically: the Hamiltonian-to-sheaf embedding is exact to machine precision, the cohomology dimension reproduces non-bonding-orbital counts across eleven conjugated molecules, the sheaf Laplacian is O(3)-equivariant to machine precision, and the equivariant model attains lower error and rotation generalization on a directional electronic target. Our contribution is this sheaf-theoretic formalization and its invariants, not equivariant Hamiltonian prediction itself.

</details>


### [10] [Data Predictability Shapes Weibull Weight-Scale Growth in Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23573)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现训练后的Transformer权重规模可用双参数Weibull分布（形状k≈1.2）刻画，并提出了一个基于大gram条件熵D的、训练前可计算的前向预测律：λ²-λ₀² = C₀(η) + C₁(η)(H_r-D)^0.59。该定律在去除每学习率系数后，跨23个运行数据点以单位斜率坍缩到同一曲线上（R²=0.941），端到端自验证恢复权重增长的相对误差仅5.7%，并指出跨语料预测对代码数据集存在过度预测，暗示冗余性是数据-权重框架的第二个轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明Transformer权重幅度可由Weibull分布概括，但关键的尺度参数λ由训练决定，其增长背后的数据侧驱动因素未知。作者希望找到一个在训练前即可计算的语料统计量，作为权重增长的前向预测器，从而建立数据属性到训练后权重形态的可解释桥梁。

Method: 使用bigram条件熵D=H(next|prev)作为训练前统计量，设计受控的语料损坏家族，拟合学习率条件化的定律λ²-λ₀²=C₀(η)+C₁(η)(H_r-D)^0.59。凸指数0.59并非直接拟合生长曲线，而是从独立测得的数据侧饱和关系中继承。随后去除每个η的系数，检验跨学习率（一个数量级变化）的23个运行是否坍缩到同一指数曲线上，并在模型级与逐层分辨率、两种架构上验证形式不变性。

Result: 定律展现了强跨运行一致性：去除每η系数后，23个运行以单位斜率坍缩到(H_r-D)^0.59上（R²=0.941），优于直接每η拟合（R²≈0.82）。端到端自验证中，对held-out同一语料家族内权重增长的恢复误差仅5.7%。该形式在两种架构、模型级与逐层分辨率下均保持，仅系数改变。但跨语料预测对代码过度预测，表明冗余性是第二维度。

Conclusion: 训练后的权重增长可以由训练前可计算的bigram条件熵以幂律形式准确预测，且该定律具跨架构、跨分辨率的泛化性。但该定律存在边界：跨语料外推失效，暗示一个更广泛的Φ(D,R,A,H)数据-权重框架，其中冗余性(R)是与熵并行的第二控制轴。

Abstract: A trained transformer's weight magnitudes can be summarized by a two-parameter Weibull distribution whose shape $k \approx 1.2$ is stable across layers and models, so the scale $λ$ carries most training-induced movement. What corpus property sets how much $λ$ grows? Using the bigram conditional entropy $D = H(\text{next} \mid \text{prev})$, a training-free statistic computed before training, we find across controlled corruption families a learning-rate-conditioned law, $λ^2 - λ_0^2 = C_0(η) + C_1(η)(H_r - D)^{0.59}$, where $H_r$ is a matched-budget shuffle baseline. The convex exponent is inherited from an independently measured data-side saturation relation rather than fitted directly to the growth curve. After removing the two per-$η$ coefficients, 23 runs spanning an order of magnitude in learning rate collapse onto $(H_r - D)^{0.59}$ with unit slope ($R^2 = 0.941$; direct per-$η$ fits are weaker, $R^2 \approx 0.82$). Because $D$ is computed before training, the law is a forward predictor: an end-to-end self-validation recovers held-out within-family weight growth with 5.7% relative error. The readout holds at model and per-layer resolutions and across two tested architectures, with the functional form preserved and only the coefficients changing. It also marks its boundary: cross-corpus prediction over-predicts code, implicating redundancy as a second axis of a broader $Φ(D,R,A,H)$ data-to-weight framework.

</details>


### [11] [From Causal Plausibility to Causal Reliability: Evaluating LLMs as Calibrated Direct Causal-Edge Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23660)
*Amit Kumar,Elnur Adl Zarabi,Suranjana Trivedy,Zhiqian Chen,Lei Zhang,Kaiqun Fu,Taoran Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了12个指令微调开源LLM在结构因果发现中提供先验因果知识的能力，发现LLM因果判断高度偏向召回（过度预测稠密图），虽然能捕捉因果相关性但难以识别直接性与方向性，且常规置信度估计（尤其logit-based）不可靠，而跨提示/跨模型一致性提供了更有前景的置信信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多被用作结构因果发现的先验因果知识来源，但其直接边判断和置信度是否可靠尚未得到系统验证。作者旨在通过严格评估回答这一问题，为LLM在因果发现中的合理使用提供依据。

Method: 对12个指令微调开源模型，在6个基准因果图、5种提示策略和4种置信度来源（言语化、logit-based、跨提示一致性、跨模型一致性）下进行系统评估。采用仅语言（language-only）的成对协议，并额外进行基准熟悉度审计（识别出5个涉及AsiaM的模型-数据集对存在潜在熟悉度问题）。

Result: 三大核心发现：（i）LLM因果判断强烈偏向召回主导，模型预测过度稠密图，大量假正边；提示主要改变精度-召回权衡而非解决过度预测，模型规模增益在大图上减弱且不能消除误校准。（ii）LLM能捕捉因果相关性，但不可靠地识别直接性/方向性——40.0%的间接边和36.0%的反转非边被误判为直接边（vs其他非边28.2%），且80.8%/84.6%的这类假正获得≥80%的言语化置信度，显示严重过度自信。（iii）logit-based置信度常崩溃至接近1.0，而跨提示和跨模型一致性取得更好的平均校准与判别力，但在Holm校正后无统计显著性。

Conclusion: LLM更适合被视为外部验证的软因果先验来源，而非因果结构的直接证据。研究者应避免依赖LLM的直接边判断与置信度输出，可考虑利用跨模型/跨提示一致性作为更可靠的信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide prior causal knowledge for structural causal discovery, yet whether their direct-edge judgments and confidence can be trusted remains unclear. We systematically evaluate 12 instruction-tuned open-weight models across six benchmark causal graphs, five prompting strategies, and four confidence sources: verbalized, logit-based, cross-prompt agreement, and cross-model agreement. Under our language-only pairwise protocol, our evaluation yields three key findings. (i) LLM-based causal judgments are strongly recall-dominant: models predict overly dense graphs with many false-positive edges, while prompting mainly shifts the precision-recall trade-off rather than resolving overprediction. Gains from model scale diminish on the largest graphs and do not eliminate miscalibration. (ii) LLMs often capture causal relatedness without reliably identifying directness or orientation. Relative to published reference graphs, models misclassify 40.0% of indirect and 36.0% of reversed non-edges as direct edges, versus 28.2% of other non-edges. Moreover, 80.8% and 84.6% of these false positives receive verbalized confidence of at least 80%, revealing substantial overconfidence in structurally incorrect predictions. (iii) Conventional confidence estimates are unreliable, whereas agreement offers a more promising signal. Logit-based confidence frequently collapses near 1.0 regardless of correctness, while cross-prompt and cross-model agreement achieve better mean calibration and discrimination, though their advantages are not statistically significant after Holm correction. A benchmark-familiarity audit further identifies potential familiarity in five model-dataset pairs, all involving AsiaM. Overall, our results suggest LLMs are better viewed as sources of externally validated soft causal priors than as direct evidence of causal structure.

</details>


### [12] [Renormalization Group Flow Matching for Scalable Local Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23696)
*Kanta Masuki,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出重正化群流匹配（RGFM），一种利用重正化群（RG）流作为概率路径的生成框架，通过从长波长到短波长结构逐步生成数据，结合准局域性和尺度分离特性，实现仅用局部计算即可捕捉长程相关性的可扩展生成模型。理论上证明了RGFM概率流可由作用范围O(Λ⁻¹[ln L+ln(1/ε)])的局部速度场精确近似，计算成本随系统体积近似线性增长；在1D分布和FFHQ图像上展示了优于传统局部流匹配的长期关联保持能力和样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型面临基本权衡：全局方法能捕捉完整结构一致性但计算成本高，局部模型高效但难以复现长程关联和全局一致性。作者希望借助重正化群在桥梁不同尺度空间结构上的天然优势，在保持计算可扩展性的同时实现全局结构一致的生成。

Method: RGFM利用精确的RG流作为概率路径，系统性地跨不同空间尺度组织数据生成过程（从长到短波长）。利用RG的两个关键性质——准局域性与尺度分离——证明RGFM概率流可由局部速度场精确近似，从而允许使用大小为O(ln L)的局部patch进行生成建模，计算成本与系统体积近似线性增长。

Result: 数值实验表明：1）在代表性一维分布中，局部RGFM能复现远超其感受野的长程相关性，而传统局部流匹配在长距离上存在显著误差；2）在FFHQ图像上，RGFM在64x64和256x256分辨率下均产生比局部流匹配更连贯、质量更高的样本。

Conclusion: RG引导的概率流为可扩展生成建模提供了有前景的途径，实现了仅使用局部计算即可捕捉长程结构的生成能力，弥合了全局建模质量与局部模型可扩展性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite their remarkable success in modeling complex data, generative models face a fundamental tradeoff. Global approaches can capture full structural coherence but suffer from high computational costs, while local models are efficient but often fail to reproduce long-range correlations and global coherence. The renormalization group (RG) bridges this gap by seamlessly connecting spatial structures across different length scales, retaining quasi-local descriptions at each step while preserving long-range correlations. We introduce renormalization group flow matching (RGFM), a generative framework that systematically structures data generation across different spatial scales. By using an exact RG flow as the probability path, RGFM progressively generates data from long- to short-wavelength structures. To reconcile scalability with global structure, we exploit two key properties of the RG: quasi-locality and scale separation. We rigorously show that the RGFM probability flow can be accurately approximated by local velocity fields acting over a spatial range $O(Λ^{-1}[\ln L+\ln(1/\varepsilon)])$ for RG wavenumber scale $Λ$, linear system size $L$, and prescribed error tolerance $\varepsilon$. This property enables local generative modeling with patches of size $O(\ln L)$ and a computational cost that scales nearly linearly with the system volume. We numerically demonstrate that local RGFM reproduces long-range correlations far beyond its receptive field in representative one-dimensional distributions, while conventional local flow matching exhibits substantial errors at long distances. On FFHQ images, RGFM yields far more coherent and higher-quality samples than local flow matching at 64x64 and 256x256. Our results establish RG-guided probability flows as a promising route toward scalable generative modeling that captures long-range structure using only local computation.

</details>


### [13] [Response Renormalization for Critical Deep Equilibrium Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23725)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出响应重归一化（Response Renormalization）框架及其两种具体实现（CMR和Phi-CMR），用于控制深度平衡模型（DEQ）在隐式微分中因残差雅可比近似奇异而产生的梯度爆炸问题。该方法在低维临界子空间内选择性提升近极点分母，同时保持良态响应通道不变。在23类多物理场、大量静态和瞬态实验中，该方法在98%（静态）和95%（瞬态）的设置下测试误差不超过精确隐式微分模型的5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEQ训练依赖隐式微分求解伴随系统，而伴随系统由残差雅可比决定。当雅可比沿损失敏感方向近似奇异时，微小的扰动会在伴随响应中被强烈放大，产生大而高度敏感的梯度，导致优化不可靠。现有方法缺少对临界方向的针对性控制。

Method: 提出Response Renormalization反向传播框架：选择性地提升接近极点（近奇异）的分母，保持未提升响应通道不变。具体包含：1）Collective Mode Response Renormalization (CMR)，在低维临界子空间内施加修正；2）Phi-adaptive CMR，基于正磁化率规则计算有界响应质量；3）推导稠密和矩阵自由两种集体形式；4）区分修正冻结锚残差的精确梯度与反向响应代理；5）扩展到结构化隐式层和矢量吸引子（SILVA）。

Result: 在23类多物理场（含偏微分方程、三维场、算子映射、复杂几何和粒子系统）上，98%以上的静态和95%以上的瞬态族-种子比较中，CMR和Phi-CMR的测试误差不超过精确隐式微分训练模型的5%。求解器指标实验显示收敛于静态伴随解，物理时间滚动在评估条件下保持预测保真度。

Conclusion: 选择性响应重归一化能够在不全局抑制良态敏感性的前提下控制近临界伴随放大，使参数更新更可靠，同时保留学习所需的有效梯度信息。

Abstract: Deep Equilibrium Models (DEQs) compute predictions from a hidden representation unchanged by the model update. Training through this equilibrium uses implicit differentiation and requires solving an adjoint system built from the residual Jacobian. If this Jacobian is nearly singular along loss-sensitive directions, small perturbations can be strongly amplified in the adjoint response, producing large, highly sensitive gradients that can make optimization unreliable. We introduce Response Renormalization, a backward-pass framework that lifts selected near-pole denominators while leaving unlifted response channels unchanged. Collective Mode Response Renormalization (CMR) applies this correction in a low-dimensional critical subspace, while Phi-adaptive CMR computes a bounded response mass from a positive susceptibility rule. We derive dense and matrix-free collective formulations, distinguish exact gradients of a modified frozen-anchor residual from backward-response surrogates, and extend the construction to Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors (SILVA). Across 23 multiphysics families spanning partial differential equations, three-dimensional fields, operator maps, complex geometries, and particle systems, CMR and Phi-CMR yield test errors no more than five percent higher than those from models trained with exact implicit differentiation in more than 98% of static and 95% of transient family-seed comparisons. Solver-index experiments show convergence toward the static adjoint, while physical-time rollouts retain predictive fidelity under the evaluated conditions. These results demonstrate that selective response renormalization can control near-critical adjoint amplification without globally damping well-conditioned sensitivity. Therefore, the method can make parameter updates more reliable while preserving the useful gradient information needed for learning.

</details>


### [14] [Calibration-Preserving Pruning: Compression as a Reliability Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23744)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Adria Binte Habib,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出校准保持剪枝（CPP）方法，通过将非一致性梯度显著性与基础剪枝分数相结合，并使用分离的数据划分（剪枝、验证选择、共形校准和测试），在保证有限样本边际覆盖的同时提高预测集的效率（即减小共形预测集的大小）。实验表明CPP-SparseGPT在DBpedia-14上可将平均集大小从10.1降至8.6，但对照实验显示通用监督梯度（如SNIP）解释了大部分收益，真实标签CPP与Wanda+SNIP差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝后的模型在共形预测中面临效率与覆盖的权衡：已有理论保证剪枝模型固定后共形预测可保持有限样本边际覆盖（由分裂共形预测提供），但剪枝是否能够保留足够的分数几何结构以生成更小的有效预测集，这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: CPP在基础剪枝分数（如SparseGPT或Wanda的权重重要性分数）基础上加入非一致性梯度显著性（nonconformity-gradient saliency），并采用四个分离的数据划分：剪枝、验证选择、共形校准和测试。理论上证明有界分数扰动可导出有界共形分位数偏移，从而控制集合膨胀；但这一界不使通用覆盖定理成为CPP特有。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B的5种子实验中，50%稀疏度下CPP在大标签任务上收益最大。DBpedia-14上CPP-SparseGPT将平均集大小从10.1降至8.6，准确率由0.347提升至0.366；CPP-Wanda将集大小从11.2降至9.0，准确率由0.310降至0.295。15个数据集-稀疏度组合中，CPP-SparseGPT在13个中产生更小集合、11个中准确率更高。但匹配对照实验表明真正的CPP与Wanda+SNIP无统计显著差异，提示通用监督梯度贡献了大部分收益；阈值感知候选标签CPP在显式准确率和离线计算成本下可达7.8的平均集大小。RoBERTa-base和Llama-3-8B的诊断结果支持迁移性，但结论限定于可靠性敏感的分类任务。

Conclusion: CPP展示了将非一致性感知信号融入剪枝以提升共形预测集效率的可行性，但对照实验揭示其核心增益主要源于通用监督梯度而非真实标签的特定信息。该方法的有效性在可靠性敏感分类场景中得到验证，其适用范围不应过度泛化到其他任务类型。

Abstract: Split conformal prediction, not the pruning rule, supplies finite-sample marginal coverage once a pruned model is fixed independently of the conformal calibration split. We study the separate efficiency problem: can pruning preserve score geometry well enough to obtain smaller valid prediction sets? Calibration-Preserving Pruning (CPP) augments a base pruning score with nonconformity-gradient saliency and uses disjoint pruning, validation-selection, conformal-calibration, and test splits. Bounded score perturbations imply bounded conformal-quantile shifts and controlled set inflation, but do not make the generic coverage theorem CPP-specific. Final five-seed Qwen2.5-1.5B results at 50\% sparsity show the largest gains on large-label tasks. On DBpedia-14, CPP-SparseGPT reduces mean set size from \(10.1\) to \(8.6\) while changing accuracy from \(0.347\) to \(0.366\); CPP-Wanda reduces \(11.2\) to \(9.0\) with an accuracy trade-off from \(0.310\) to \(0.295\). Across 15 dataset--sparsity cells, CPP-SparseGPT produces smaller sets in 13 and higher accuracy in 11. Matched controls show that generic supervised gradients explain much of the gain: true-label CPP is not statistically resolved from matched Wanda+SNIP, whereas threshold-aware candidate-label CPP reaches \(7.8\) mean set size at explicit accuracy and offline-compute costs. RoBERTa-base and Llama-3-8B diagnostics support transfer, but our claims remain limited to reliability-sensitive classification.

</details>


### [15] [Delayed Optimizer-State Transport Shapes Short-Horizon Training Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24593)
*Jinhui Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应优化器（AdamW）中动量状态对短期决策的延迟传输效应。通过在12个未使用的0.3M Transformer模型历史中比较八步AdamW轨迹的完整传输与即时导数方法，发现完整传输在10/12个历史中降低了token不相交损失（平均收益4.71×10⁻⁴，p=0.0193）。交叉检查点-未来路径测试表明排序重排源于优化器状态与近未来数据的交互，独立的Ising-CNN实验证实删除动量状态传输会破坏准确响应预测。研究结论是优化器记忆和近未来数据顺序是可操作的训练状态组件，提供了需要有限视界而非单步干预的机制性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器在动量变量中保留梯度历史，这使得损失加权的局部变化能够影响后续更新。作者想要检验这种延迟传输是否足够大到改变短期视界的决策，从而为是否需要有限视界而非单步干预提供机制性判据。

Method: 在固定的未来mini-batch序列上，对八步AdamW轨迹通过完整的模型-优化器状态进行求导，选择暴露匹配的Math-Code损失调度，然后独立评估。具体包括：1)在12个未使用的0.3M Transformer历史中比较完整状态传输与优化器感知的即时导数方法；2)进行交叉检查点-未来路径测试以归因排序重排的原因；3)独立进行Ising-CNN实验，删除动量状态传输以验证其必要性；4)在更大的候选库中测试完整传输分数对精确rollout获胜者的集中能力。

Result: 完整传输在10/12个Transformer历史中优于即时导数方法（平均收益4.71×10⁻⁴；精确单侧符号检验p=0.0193）。两个控制器作用频率相同但在60/96个窗口中选择了不同调度。交叉测试将重排归因于优化器状态与近未来数据的交互。Ising-CNN实验显示删除动量状态传输会破坏准确响应预测。在更大候选库中，完整传输分数能集中精确rollout的获胜者，实现有限振幅评估的短名单聚焦。

Conclusion: 在提交的短路径上，优化器记忆和近未来数据顺序是训练状态中可操作的组成部分。该工作提供了基于机制的判据，用于判断何时需要有限视界干预而非单步干预，对理解自适应优化器的动态行为及训练干预策略设计具有参考价值。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers retain gradient history in moment variables, allowing a local change in loss weighting to alter later updates. We examine whether this delayed transport is large enough to change prospective short-horizon decisions. On committed future-minibatch sequences, we differentiate eight-step AdamW trajectories through the complete model--optimizer state and select exposure-matched Math--Code loss schedules before independent evaluation. Across 12 unused 0.3M Transformer histories, full transport lowers token-disjoint loss relative to an optimizer-aware immediate derivative in 10/12 histories (mean benefit $4.71\times10^{-4}$; exact one-sided sign test, $p=0.0193$). The two controllers act equally often but select different schedules in 60/96 windows. Crossed checkpoint--future-path tests attribute this reordering to the interaction between optimizer state and near-future data, while an independent Ising--CNN experiment shows that deleting moment-state transport destroys accurate response prediction. Full-transport scores also concentrate exact-rollout winners in larger candidate libraries, focusing finite-amplitude evaluation on a shortlist. On these committed short paths, optimizer memory and near-future data order are therefore actionable components of the training state, providing a mechanism-based criterion for when finite-horizon rather than one-step intervention is required.

</details>


### [16] [Tight Majorizations and Convergence Rates of Nuclear Norm Minimization IRLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23765)
*Christian Kümmerle,Tomas Masak,Dominik Stöger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了约束核范数最小化中IRLS方法的锐利收敛率分析，证明了调和均值权重算子定义了有效的全局二次majorizer，且在该类方法中是最优的，并证明了使用该权重的IRLS算法具有维度无关的局部线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRLS方法被广泛用于核范数最小化，但其收敛率和权重算子的作用长期未被深入理解，尤其是权重算子的选择对收敛性能的影响缺乏理论解释。

Method: 通过新的majorization分析框架分析平滑核范数，证明调和均值权重算子构成全局二次majorizer；利用Schatten-1零空间性质证明多种权重算子的IRLS算法全局线性收敛；通过构造反例证明单边权重算子的IRLS算法无法获得维度无关的局部线性收敛率。

Result: 证明了调和均值权重算子在幂均值权重族中的最优性；在Schatten-1零空间性质下证明了多种权重算子的IRLS全局线性收敛；对调和均值权重IRLS证明了维度无关的局部线性收敛率，并给出单边权重无法达到该速率的反例；数值实验验证了理论结果及调和均值加权的实际优势。

Conclusion: 调和均值权重是IRLS核范数最小化中更优的权重算子选择，优于文献中主流的单边加权方案，能够在低秩恢复中实现更快的收敛保证。

Abstract: Iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS) methods constitute a natural approach to nuclear norm minimization, but their convergence rates and the role of the weight operator have remained poorly understood. This paper establishes sharp convergence rates for IRLS methods for constrained nuclear norm minimization in low-rank recovery. A central ingredient is a new majorization analysis for the smoothed nuclear norm: we prove that the harmonic-mean weight operator defines a valid global quadratic majorizer. Furthermore, we show that this weight operator is optimal within the family of power-mean weights, clarifying why it improves over classical one-sided reweighting schemes that use only row- or column-space information. Under a Schatten-1 null space property, we prove global linear convergence of IRLS algorithms using a variety of weight operators, including the harmonic-mean weights. For IRLS with harmonic-mean weights, we prove a dimension-independent, locally linear convergence rate. We provide a counterexample showing that this dimension-independent local rate cannot in general be obtained for IRLS algorithms using one-sided weight operators, which predominate in the literature. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and illustrate the practical advantage of harmonic-mean reweighting across square, rectangular, and adversarially initialized recovery problems.

</details>


### [17] [Disentangled Skill Representations for Predictive Human Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23776)
*Mariah Schrum,Deepak Gopinath,Srijan Srivatsa,Guy Rosman,Tiffany Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIL方法，将人类技能建模为可解释的多维潜在构造，通过持久技能嵌入控制专家与新手基底的混合，并利用反事实子技能交换实现解纠缠，从而在赛车和棒球领域实现稳健的行为预测与AI辅导提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与依赖于单一观测的典型潜变量估计问题不同，技能是一种持久的、组合性的、以行为为基础的构造，需要从随时间变化的行为模式中进行推断。理解人类技能对协作、辅导和辅助人类的AI系统至关重要。

Method: SAIL使用持久技能嵌入来表示每个个体，该嵌入控制专家与新手基底的混合；通过反事实子技能交换进行解纠缠训练，以鼓励表示既对表现波动鲁棒又结构可解释。

Result: 在赛车和棒球两个领域，SAIL在预测性能上表现强劲，在行为学基础的解纠缠指标上持续优于所评估的基线方法，并同时改善了下游AI辅导性能。

Conclusion: SAIL证明技能可以被建模为可解释、多维、持久且可迁移的表示，不仅能提升行为预测的泛化能力，还能在AI辅导等下游任务中带来实际改进，验证了将技能视为持久组合性构造的研究方向有效性。

Abstract: Understanding human skill is important for AI systems that collaborate with, coach, or assist people. Unlike typical latent variable estimation problems which rely on single observations, skill is a persistent, compositional, and behaviorally grounded construct that must be inferred from patterns over time. We introduce Skill Abstraction with Interpretable Latents (SAIL), a method for modeling human skill as an interpretable, multi-dimensional construct inferred from naturalistic behavior. Our approach produces a skill embedding that is robust to transient performance fluctuations and learns a transferable representation of human subskills. Furthermore, SAIL supports skill-informed behavior prediction that generalizes across a variety of in-domain contexts. We represent each individual with a persistent skill embedding that controls a blend between expert and novice bases and is trained using counterfactual subskill swaps for disentanglement. This design encourages representations that are both robust to performance variation and structured for interpretability. We demonstrate across racing and baseball that SAIL achieves strong predictive performance and consistently improves behaviorally grounded disentanglement over the evaluated baselines, while also improving downstream AI coaching performance.

</details>


### [18] [GAP-Prompt: Gated Adaptive Prompting for Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23782)
*Trung-Anh Dang,Duy-Cuong Bui,Ngoc-Son Vu,Christel Vrain,Vincent Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAP-Prompt，一种为持续学习设计的实例级自适应提示方法，通过动态门控（选择最优提示注入层）、动态知识融合（实例感知地聚合当前与历史提示）和共享提示蒸馏（在早期共享层锚定基础知识）三个模块协同工作，在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R和CUB-200基准上达到最先进性能，在细粒度CUB-200上取得87.29%精度，接近联合训练上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的持续学习方法通常依赖静态的任务级提示策略，忽视了任务内部细粒度的样本多样性（intra-task diversity），导致无法充分适应不同输入样本的具体需求。

Method: 提出GAP-Prompt框架，包含三个协同模块：(1) 实例条件门控（instance-conditioned gating）——为每张图像动态决定最优的提示注入层；(2) 动态知识融合（dynamic knowledge fusion）——进行实例感知的当前与历史提示聚合，实现跨任务知识整合；(3) 共享提示蒸馏（shared prompt distillation）——在早期共享层中锚定基础知识以缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R和CUB-200三个基准上持续取得最先进性能；在细粒度CUB-200数据集上达到87.29%的准确率，接近联合训练上界（88.00%），显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: GAP-Prompt通过将提示机制从任务级细化为实例级，有效解决了静态任务级提示忽视样本内多样性的问题，同时通过知识融合与蒸馏，在保持新任务学习能力的同时显著缓解了灾难性遗忘，验证了实例级自适应提示在持续学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning faces the persistent challenge of catastrophic forgetting, where sequential task updates degrade previously acquired knowledge. While prompt-based methods integrated with pre-trained models offer a compelling solution by freezing the backbone, they often rely on static, task-level prompting strategies that overlook fine-grained intra-task diversity. In this paper, we propose Gated Adaptive Prompting (GAP-Prompt), a novel method that introduces instance-level adaptability to the prompting process. GAP-Prompt consists of three synergistic modules: (1) instance-conditioned gating, which dynamically determines optimal prompt injection layers for each individual image; (2) dynamic knowledge fusion, which performs instance-aware aggregation of current and historical prompts, enabling knowledge integration across tasks; and (3) shared prompt distillation, which anchors foundational knowledge in early shared layers to mitigate forgetting. Extensive evaluations on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and CUB-200 benchmarks demonstrate that GAP-Prompt consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, on the fine-grained CUB-200 dataset, GAP-Prompt reaches 87.29% accuracy, approaching the joint training upper bound (88.00%) and outperforming existing methods by a significant margin.

</details>


### [19] [Mixture of Channel Experts: Static Sparse Supports with Input-Adaptive Mixing for Pointwise Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23794)
*Elian Iluk,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture of Channel Experts (MoCE)，一种受MoE启发的结构化稀疏通道混合层，将专家从算子复制改为通道选择，用输入预测温度参数的softmax聚合选中的k个输入通道，以替换计算成本为二次方的点卷积投影。MoCE将相对成本降至k/C，在ResNet、EfficientViT等架构上匹配或超越稠密基线，同时减少16.7%的MACs和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现将MoE设计直接复制到卷积网络中会因结构性原因失败：读取相同输入通道的并行卷积专家会学习到几乎相同的滤波器。因此需要重新设计MoE在卷积网络中的专家定义方式，将专家轴从算子重复转向通道选择，以实现高效且有效的稀疏通道混合。

Method: MoCE将每个输出通道视为一个专家，每个专家学习一个稀疏支持集（k个输入通道，k远小于通道总数C）。选中的通道通过softmax聚合，其温度参数由每个输入预测，使专家可在均值式和最大值式聚合间自适应切换。此外设置一个残差专家汇总未选中的通道，并采用负载均衡损失保证通道覆盖的完整性。MoCE替代了成本随C二次增长的点卷积投影。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和CIFAR-100上的ResNet骨干网络、迁移学习、EfficientViT以及现代训练策略等多个场景中，MoCE匹配或超越了稠密基线和先前的通道选择方法，同时将MACs降低16.7%，并在真实墙钟时间中验证了预测的加速收益。

Conclusion: MoCE证明了将MoE思想从算子复制迁移到通道选择在卷积网络中是有效的，能实现与传统密集投影相当甚至更优的性能，同时显著降低计算成本和延迟，为卷积网络的稀疏化设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales language models by routing each input through a small set of independently parameterized experts. We show that copying this design into convolutional networks fails for a structural reason: parallel convolutional experts that read the same input channels learn nearly identical filters. We therefore move the expert axis from operator duplication to channel selection. We introduce Mixture of Channel Experts (MoCE), a structured sparse channel-mixing layer, inspired by MoE, that replaces pointwise (1x1) channel-reduction projections. In MoCE, an expert is a single output channel with a learned sparse support of k << C input channels. The selected channels are combined by a softmax whose temperature is predicted per input, so each expert can move between mean-like and max-like aggregation. A residual expert summarizes the unselected channels, and a load-balancing loss keeps channel coverage complete. MoCE replaces a dense projection whose cost is quadratic in C with a mechanism whose relative cost scales as k/C, and the predicted savings hold in measured wall-clock time. Across ResNet backbones on ImageNet-1K and CIFAR-100, transfer learning, EfficientViT, and a strong modern training recipe, MoCE matches or exceeds dense baselines and prior channel-selection methods while reducing MACs by 16.7% and end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [20] [Revelation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23860)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Revelation Control"（揭示控制）理论，用于解决"选择定价干预以仅揭示能改变关键决策的隐藏状态"的问题，并区分干预本身产生的有用进展。该理论为学习系统定义了决策充分的揭示与揭示深度，分离纯信息价值与生产性重用，将静态贝叶斯细化嵌入状态依赖的延续价值，并给出精确的成本调整分解准则。实验在Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B-v0.3上验证了理论的适用性与系统特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际决策系统中，干预（如训练、探查）会揭示隐藏状态，但并非所有揭示的信息都能改变最终决策。传统方法未能区分"仅因信息价值而值得揭示"与"因干预本身创造生产性进展而值得执行"的区别，也没有系统性地处理信息揭示的成本与决策价值之间的权衡。

Method: 开发了Revelation Control理论框架，包括：(1)定义决策充分的揭示与揭示深度；(2)分离纯信息价值与生产性重用；(3)将静态贝叶斯细化嵌入状态依赖的延续价值；(4)给出精确的成本调整分解准则——当共享标量摘要的状态位于定价的Stop/Continue边界两侧时，额外浅层坐标才是决策非冗余的；(5)提供目标无关的模型特定实例化协议，并证明有界stop-flip风险无法在无限制严重性下保证正期望效用。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B-v0.3上的实验表明：更深的未来学习探查具有正的决策价值；生产性重用产生严格的等计算效用优势。Qwen提供了决策非冗余浅层可揭示性机制的证据；Mistral中标量延续架构仅在独立开发面板上拟合，在不相交的目标面板上仍保持正的家庭方式调整下界，在测试的架构族和分辨率内一致于标量决策充分性。证据支持结构化而非数值化的迁移。

Conclusion: 该理论框架（决策理论、成本核算、延续逻辑和评估协议）可跨系统迁移，但经验代理、系数、阈值乃至所需浅层状态维度都可能因系统而异。即理论结构是可迁移的，而数值参数是系统特定的。有界stop-flip风险不足以在无限制严重性下保证正期望效用，说明需要更严格的认证条件。

Abstract: Revelation Control is the problem of choosing priced interventions that reveal hidden state only insofar as the revealed distinctions can change a consequential decision, while accounting separately for any useful progress created by the intervention itself. We develop this theory for learning systems, where states equivalent under declared current information can respond differently to future training and favor different actions. The framework defines decision-sufficient revelation and revelation depth, separates pure information value from productive reuse, embeds static Bayes refinement into state-dependent continuation value, and gives an exact cost-adjusted factorization criterion: an additional shallow coordinate is decision-nonredundant only when states sharing a scalar summary lie on opposite sides of the priced Stop/Continue boundary. We also give a target-independent protocol for model-specific instantiation and prove that bounded stop-flip risk alone cannot certify positive expected utility under unrestricted severity. Across Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, deeper future-learning probes have positive decision value and productive reuse yields strict equal-compute utility advantages. Qwen additionally provides evidence for a decision-nonredundant shallow revealability regime; in Mistral, a scalar continuation architecture fit only on an independent development panel retains positive familywise-adjusted lower bounds on a disjoint target panel, consistent with scalar decision sufficiency within the tested architecture family and resolution. The evidence supports structural rather than numerical transfer: the decision theory, cost accounting, continuation logic, and evaluation protocol transport, while empirical proxies, coefficients, thresholds, and even the required shallow state dimension may be system-specific.

</details>


### [21] [A Theory of Speciation in Generative Diffusion Models on Compact Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23798)
*Alessio Marta,Paola Causin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩散模型中"物种形成"(speciation)的内在理论，在紧致黎曼流形上通过概率密度临界点的分岔来刻画物种形成事件。通过热核谱表示揭示流形几何的作用，利用Poincaré-Hopf和Morse理论对分数平衡点的数量和类型施加全局约束。对于热核混合模型，证明了generic物种形成事件具有一维临界核并服从A2折叠正规型；对称配置下出现叉形分岔和多方向转变。同时推导了几何依赖的物种形成时间估计，证明了非退化折叠在分数扰动下的结构稳定性，并在球面和真实数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型物种形成理论通常将物种形成等同于对称叉形分岔(symmetric pitchfork bifurcation)，并且假设在高维空间中进行推导。这种描述过于简化，无法捕捉流形几何对物种形成过程的影响，也忽略了拓扑约束带来的全局效应。作者旨在建立一种超越传统描述的内在理论，将物种形成与流形拓扑和几何结构联系起来。

Method: 作者将物种形成刻画为演化概率密度临界点的分岔。具体方法包括：(1) 利用谱热核表示显式揭示流形几何的作用；(2) 应用Poincaré-Hopf和Morse理论推导分数平衡点的全局数量和类型约束，揭示拓扑强制的几何模式；(3) 对热核混合模型，证明generic物种形成的临界核是一维的，并具有A2折叠正规型；(4) 推导双峰混合和黎曼正则单纯形上的几何依赖物种形成时间估计；(5) 通过结构稳定性分析证明非退化折叠在分数扰动下的鲁棒性，并确定一阶时间偏移由临界方向上的分数误差分量决定；(6) 在球面上用von Mises-Fisher混合分布验证理论，并设计基于神经网络的图集内在分数学习方案在原型和复杂数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明generic物种形成事件呈现A2折叠分岔而非对称叉形分岔，后者仅出现在非generic的对称配置中。同时获得了与拓扑相关的几何模式、几何依赖的物种形成时间估计以及分数扰动的鲁棒性保证。在球面von Mises-Fisher混合上观察到了叉形分岔、鞍结分岔、拓扑模式和多级物种形成现象，实验结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文建立了扩散模型物种形成的内在几何理论，表明物种形成本质上是概率密度临界点的分岔现象，受流形拓扑和几何结构约束。该理论超越了传统的大维叉形分岔描述，为理解扩散模型的生成动力学提供了更深层的数学框架。

Abstract: Speciation in generative diffusion models denotes the emergence of distinct stable branches during denoising, through which initially undifferentiated trajectories progressively commit to different data classes. In this work we develop an intrinsic theory of speciation for diffusion models supported on compact Riemannian manifolds: the aim is to go beyond existing theoretical descriptions, which usually identify speciation with a symmetric pitchfork bifurcation and assume to work in a large-dimensional space. We characterize speciation by bifurcations of the critical points of the evolving probability density. A spectral heat-kernel representation makes explicit the role of the manifold geometry, while Poincaré-Hopf and Morse theory impose global constraints on the number and type of score equilibria and reveal topologically-imposed geometrical modes. For mixtures of heat kernels, we prove that generic speciation events have a one-dimensional critical kernel and admit an A2 fold normal form; pitchforks and simultaneous multidirectional transitions arise from nongeneric symmetric configurations. We derive geometry-dependent estimates of speciation times for bimodal mixtures and Riemannian regular simplices. We further establish structural stability of nondegenerate folds under score perturbations and show that the first-order time shift is determined solely by the component of the score error along the critical direction. The theory is illustrated on the sphere using mixtures of von Mises-Fisher distributions, where pitchfork and saddle-node bifurcations, topological modes, and hierarchical multiple speciations are observed. Finally, a chart-based intrinsic score-learning scheme based on neural networks contrasts the theoretically predicted transitions on prototypal and more complex datasets.

</details>


### [22] [The Loss Floor of Denoising Score Matching: Fisher Geometry from Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23916)
*Avinash Raju,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明去噪分数匹配的训练损失中一个不可约的"过剩项"（损失下限）恰好等于条件端点的 Fisher–Rao 度量的迹沿扩散轨迹的积分，从而给出去噪目标的精确条件方差分解，并通过 Schrödinger 桥变分原理推导该结果，指出该 Fisher 项与数据丢失互信息的速率成正比。文中进一步在 Gaussian 情形给出闭式解、恢复重参数化不变性，并将高信噪比发散与数据的信息维度联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪分数匹配在训练时回归的是条件分数，而生成时需要边际分数。尽管两者在总体上有相同的最小化器，条件目标在固定噪声状态下仍是随机的，给训练损失带来了不可约的过剩（损失下限），现有理论尚未精确刻画这一过剩项的结构及其对训练行为（如不同噪声范围/加权下模型排名不一致）的深层影响。

Method: 作者从 Schrödinger 桥变分原理出发，将理想目标表示为路径空间相对熵的过剩项；在一般损坏核的温和正则假设下，证明该过剩项精确等于条件端点族 Fisher–Rao 度量迹沿扩散轨迹的积分。对 Gaussian 情形推导闭式损失下限，并将高信噪比发散与数据的信息维度建立联系。

Result: 得到去噪目标的精确条件方差分解——训练损失下限 = Fisher–Rao 度量的积分，其与噪声状态对干净数据互信息损失速率成正比，数据项与安排/目标权重项可分离。Gaussian 情形下给出闭式形式，恢复连续时间目标的重参数化不变性，高信噪比发散关联数据信息维度。此外发现不同噪声范围/加权下的原始损失因含不同可加性下限而无法一致地排序模型，并将训练中的二阶几何与数值采样误差中的三阶条件统计区分开来。

Conclusion: 该工作以精确的几何量（Fisher–Rao 度量）刻画了扩散模型训练损失的内在信息几何结构，阐明了损失下限的物理来源及其对模型比较的影响，为理解扩散模型训练与评估的理论基础提供了新的精确视角。

Abstract: Denoising score matching trains diffusion models by regressing onto a conditional score, although generation ultimately requires the marginal score. The two objectives share the same population minimizer, but the conditional target remains random at fixed noisy state and introduces an irreducible excess in the training loss. We isolate this excess and show that, for a general corruption kernel under mild regularity assumptions, it is exactly the trace of the Fisher--Rao metric of the conditional endpoint family, integrated along the diffusion trajectory. This gives an exact conditional-variance decomposition of the denoising objective and identifies the information geometry observed in diffusion latent spaces as an intrinsic component of the training loss. We derive the result from a Schr"odinger bridge variational principle, in which the ideal objective arises as excess path-space relative entropy. For corruption diffusions, the Fisher term is proportional to the rate at which the noisy state loses mutual information about the clean data, separating the loss floor into an information flow determined by the data and a weight determined by the corruption schedule and objective. In the Gaussian case, this yields a closed form for the floor, recovers reparametrization invariance of the continuous-time objective, and relates its high-SNR divergence to the information dimension of the data. Finally, we show that raw losses obtained with different noise ranges or weightings need not rank models consistently because they contain different additive floors, and contrast the second-order geometry seen by training with the third-order conditional statistics entering numerical sampling error.

</details>


### [23] [Revenge of Monosemanticity: Specialized Neurons Improve Data Efficiency in MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24007)
*Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Enric Boix-Adsera,Fivos Kalogiannis,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多层感知机（MLP）在回归任务中并非学习单一的全局低维表示，而是天然发展出"单一语义专用神经元"（monosemantic specialized neurons），即单个神经元与特定输入区域相关的预测特征强对齐，从而学习一组局部低维表示，这些表示可共同跨越高维空间，并在数据效率上优于基于全局低维表示的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征学习理论大多聚焦于全局低维预测几何结构的涌现，但作者认为这一图景并不完整，需要深入理解神经网络在实际（聚类数据）回归问题中如何组织特征。

Method: 在具有聚类数据的回归问题中，通过理论分析证明MLP会自然地形成单一语义专用神经元，即每个神经元与特定输入区域相关的预测特征强对齐，从而学习一组局部低维表示而非单一全局低维表示。

Result: 证明MLP学习的局部低维表示集合可共同跨越高维空间，且这种专用化（specialization）在数据效率上可证明地优于基于全局低维表示的特征学习方法。

Conclusion: MLP的特征学习机制比现有的全局低维表示理论所描述的更为丰富：它通过局部低维表示的集合来实现数据高效的学习，这对理解神经网络内部表示组织方式具有重要意义。

Abstract: Understanding how neural networks learn and organize features is central to understanding their behavior. Much existing theory of feature learning has focused on the emergence of a global low-dimensional predictive geometry. We show that this picture is incomplete. In regression problems with clustered data, we demonstrate that multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) naturally develop monosemantic specialized neurons: individual neurons become strongly aligned with a specific predictive feature relevant to a particular region of the input space. Rather than learning a single global low-dimensional representation, MLPs learn a collection of local low-dimensional representations that can collectively span a high-dimensional space. This specialization provably gives MLPs a data-efficiency advantage over feature-learning methods based on a global low-dimensional representation.

</details>


### [24] [Discovering Cross-Language Reasoning Invariance in LLMs with Geometry-Invariant Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23809)
*Igor Bogdanov,Changcheng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨多语言语言模型在解决同一数学问题时是否依赖共享特征或语言特定计算。在五个模型（四个系列）上使用MGSM数据集，通过CKA定位跨语言对齐层，并在选定层训练基线SAE和新提出的Geometry-Invariant SAE (GI-SAE)。GI-SAE通过加入InfoNCE损失促使编码器对同一问题的不同语言轨迹产生相似激活。通过在前向传播中交换特征值并测量KL散度来测试特征的功能可互换性。结果发现跨语言特征共享具有模型和架构依赖性，出现在不同深度；GI-SAE主要强化已存在的跨语言结构，Qwen得到加强，Gemma无功能收益，Llama和Phi呈现混合且依赖于层的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型在解决相同数学问题时，其内部计算是共享的还是语言特定的仍然不清楚。理解这一点对于可解释性和多语言模型的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 使用MGSM数据集，在六种语言中保留有效推理轨迹；先用CKA识别跨语言对齐层；在选定层分别训练基线SAE和引入InfoNCE损失的GI-SAE；通过跨语言swap特征值并测量KL散度来评估功能可互换性；并计算CKA和Jaccard相似度作为几何相似性指标。

Result: GI-SAE在几乎所有层上获得更高的CKA和Jaccard相似度，但更高的几何相似性并不一致地意味着更大的功能可互换性。跨语言特征共享的深度依模型而异，具有模型和架构依赖性。GI-SAE的效果是模型特定的：Qwen得到强化，Gemma无功能收益，Llama和Phi呈现混合且层依赖的效果。

Conclusion: 跨语言特征共享的程度和方式高度依赖具体模型和架构；GI-SAE主要放大了模型中已存在的跨语言结构而非创造新的共享特征；几何对齐与功能互换性之间不存在简单的一致关系。

Abstract: Multilingual language models can solve the same mathematical problem in different languages, but it remains unclear whether they rely on shared features or on language-specific computations that only produce similar outputs. We study this question in five models from four families using the Multilingual Grade School Math (MGSM) dataset, with problems solved in English, German, French, Spanish, Russian, and Chinese, retaining problems with valid reasoning traces in all six languages and replaying those traces through the model to record representations at multiple layers. For each model, we first use Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to identify layers with cross-language alignment. At each selected layer, we train two sparse autoencoders (SAE): a baseline reconstruction-only model and a contrastive variant introduced in this work, the Geometry-Invariant SAE (GI-SAE). GI-SAE supplements the reconstruction loss with an Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) loss that trains the encoder to produce similar activations for traces of the same problem, regardless of language or token position. We then test whether the resulting shared features are functionally interchangeable by swapping their values between languages during the model's forward pass and measuring the resulting change in output, quantified by Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence per feature. Although GI-SAE yields higher CKA and Jaccard similarity at nearly every layer, higher geometric similarity does not consistently imply greater functional interchangeability. We find that cross-language feature sharing is model- and architecture-dependent in this sample and appears at different depths in different models. GI-SAE primarily amplifies cross-language structure already present: the pattern is model-specific, with strengthening in Qwen, no functional benefit in Gemma, and mixed layer-dependent effects in Llama and Phi.

</details>


### [25] [ChorusTIC: Training-Free Multivariate Time Series Classification via Chorus In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24033)
*Juntao Fang,Shifeng Xie,Ruichu Cai,Shengji Zheng,Zijian Li,Keli Zhang,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChorusTIC是一个面向时间序列分类的原生基础模型，通过上下文学习实现跨异构通道配置的零样本分类，无需为目标任务更新参数。模型结合随机子通道槽拼接与共享双轴编码器，将可变通道配置映射为固定宽度表示，并仅基于合成带标签episode预训练，在UEA-30和UCR-128基准上展现出强全上下文与低标注性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序基础模型主要支持预测和可迁移表征学习，但分类任务仍需在目标数据集上拟合任务特定的分类器。此外，多变量输入的各个通道常被独立编码，忽视了跨通道交互信息。作者旨在构建一个分类原生的基础模型，使模型无需目标任务参数更新即能在异构通道配置下进行上下文内分类。

Method: ChorusTIC包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用episode一致的随机子通道槽拼接(Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation)将不同通道配置统一处理；(2) 共享双轴编码器(dual-axis encoder)同时建模时间交互与跨通道交互，将可变通道配置映射为与原始通道数无关的固定宽度表示；(3) 利用上下文分布校准特征轴，并通过防泄漏的上下文学习预测查询标签。模型仅在由上下文集和查询集组成的合成带标签episode上进行预训练，类别通过稀疏时间规则或跨通道规则区分。

Result: 在完整的UEA-30多变量时间序列档案和UCR-128单变量时间序列档案上的全面评估表明，ChorusTIC在无需目标任务特定分类器拟合的情况下，展现出优异的全上下文（full-context）性能和低标注（low-label）性能。

Conclusion: ChorusTIC证明了仅通过合成数据预训练即可实现跨异构通道配置的分类原生上下文学习，消除了目标任务上自定义分类器拟合的需求，为时间序列分类基础模型开辟了新范式。

Abstract: Time series classification underpins applications in healthcare, sensing, and industrial monitoring. Although time series foundation models support forecasting and transferable representation learning, classification still typically requires fitting a task-specific classifier on each target dataset, while individual channels of multivariate inputs are often encoded independently. We introduce ChorusTIC, a classification-native foundation model for in-context classification across heterogeneous channel configurations without target-task parameter updates. ChorusTIC combines episode-consistent Random Subchannel Slot Concatenation with a shared dual-axis encoder to model temporal and cross-channel interactions and map variable channel configurations into a fixed-width representation independent of the original channel count. It then calibrates feature axes using context-derived distributions and predicts query labels through leakage-protected in-context learning. We pretrain ChorusTIC solely on synthetic labeled episodes comprising context and query sets that share a task background, with classes distinguished by sparse temporal or cross-channel rules. Evaluations on the complete UEA-30 and UCR-128 archives show strong full-context and low-label performance without target-specific classifier fitting.

</details>

*Olga Manakina,Igor Bogdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的成本感知提示词选择框架，在IELTS写作评分任务中自适应选择最优提示策略，在保持与穷举网格搜索相当的评分准确度的同时，将LLM调用次数减少78.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在自动作文评分（AES）中表现强大，但当前方法通常采用固定的提示词选择，未能解决运营成本问题和最优配置动态变化的需求。

Method: 将每种提示类型视为多臂老虎机控制器中的一个"臂"，在推理过程中自适应选择最优提示策略。实现了四种评分方案（多步vs单步评估、有无校准示例），并构建成本-可靠性学习曲线，同时追踪token使用量、延迟和一致性指标。

Result: MAB框架在IELTS写作任务2上达到与穷举网格搜索相当的评分准确度，同时将LLM调用减少了78.4%。多步带校准示例的评分方案准确度最高。

Conclusion: 这是首次将在线控制机制应用于AES中自适应选择提示策略，将提示词选择从离线超参数优化问题转化为高效的在线学习任务，为需要平衡运营成本与评估有效性的教育技术平台提供了可操作的洞见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated essay scoring (AES), but contemporary approaches typically employ fixed prompt selection, failing to address operational cost concerns and evolving optimal configurations. We propose a novel cost-aware approach that treats each prompt type as an arm in a multi-armed bandit (MAB) controller, enabling adaptive selection of optimal prompting strategies during inference. Our experiments on IELTS Writing Task 2 essays show that the MAB framework achieves comparable scoring accuracy to exhaustive grid search while reducing LLM calls by 78.4\% to find the best grading approach. We implemented four distinct grading recipes (multi-step vs. single-step assessment, with vs. without calibration examples) and found that the multi-step approach with examples achieves the highest accuracy. By tracking token usage and latency alongside agreement metrics, we produce the first cost-reliability learning curves for essay scoring, providing actionable insights for educational technology platforms that must balance operational costs against assessment validity. This work represents the first application of online control mechanisms to adaptively select prompting strategies in AES, transforming prompt selection from an offline hyperparameter optimization problem into an efficient online learning task.

</details>


### [27] [Knowing When to Ask for Help: Bayesian Self-Escalation in Hierarchical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24087)
*Nadeem Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体系统中的第三种委派机制：在推理过程中实时识别自身可能失败并主动移交控制权给更强模型，即"代内委派"。作者将其形式化为一个基于学习得到的能力后验的贝叶斯最优停止问题，推导出闭式保守升级阈值，通过动态规划刻画最优策略并证明其为时变阈值。理论结果包括指数级分离、后悔界和1/sqrt(n)有限样本保证，模拟实验和真实模型验证（Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B级联）均支持理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统要么在推理开始前由路由器选择模型（委派先于推理），要么在完整响应后由验证器打分并决定是否重试（委派后于推理）。作者探索第三种未被研究的模式：智能体在自身推理过程中就意识到成功概率低，从而中途将控制权转移给更强模型，以期在推理中途更早、更节约地纠正错误路径。

Method: 将代内委派建模为贝叶斯最优停止问题，基于一个学习的"能力后验"（competence posterior）——即在线估计智能体最终任务成功概率，其充分统计量从标注轨迹中学习而非直接读取原始熵。推导闭式保守升级阈值，用动态规划刻画最优策略并证明其为时变阈值（对原始信号形状无假设）；并给出指数分离界、由后验校准控制的后悔界，以及n条标注校准轨迹下plug-in策略后悔按1/sqrt(n)衰减的有限样本保证。

Result: 受控模拟实验证实了包括1/sqrt(n)速率在内的全部理论预测。真实模型验证（Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B代码级联，MBPP 257任务）确认了两个预先注册预测中的两项：升级前沿面在同成本下支配事后路由（post-hoc routing），以及累积能力信念的判别力随生成过程上升。

Conclusion: 代内委派是一种可行的第三种委派范式，理论上具有严格的最优性和收敛保证，实践上在真实级联中优于事后路由，展示了在推理中途动态转移控制权的价值。

Abstract: Current LLM agent systems decide delegation before reasoning begins (a router picks a model) or after a response is complete (a verifier scores it and may retry). We study a third regime: an agent that recognises, during its own reasoning, that it is unlikely to succeed and transfers control to a stronger model. We formulate intra-generation delegation as a Bayesian optimal-stopping problem over a learned competence posterior -- an online estimate of the agent's eventual task success whose sufficient statistics are learned from labelled trajectories, not read off raw entropy. We derive the myopic escalation threshold in closed form, characterise the optimal policy via dynamic programming, and prove that the optimal policy is a time-varying threshold with no shape assumption on the raw signal. We further prove exponential separation of the oracle belief at the Chernoff-information rate of the signal, a regret bound governed by the calibration of the posterior, and a finite-sample guarantee: with n labelled calibration trajectories the deployed plug-in policy's regret decays as 1/sqrt(n). A controlled simulation study confirms each prediction of the theory, including the predicted 1/sqrt(n) rate. We additionally report a real-model validation on a Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B->7B code cascade (MBPP, 257 tasks), confirming two of three pre-registered predictions: the escalation frontier dominates post-hoc routing at equal cost, and the cumulative competence belief's discrimination rises over generation.

</details>


### [28] [AQLoRA: A Zero-Search Recipe for Fast Quantized LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23816)
*Md Romyull Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AQLoRA是一种自适应量化LoRA微调方法，通过单次CPU遍历按NF4重建误差对层排名，在内存预算下将前K层保留为fp16以跳过反量化，从而在保持QLoRA精度的同时提升训练速度。速度模式训练比QLoRA快11.1±2.7%（损失约1个精度点），质量模式快4.8±2.4%且精度与QLoRA持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLoRA虽然节省了内存但并没有节省时间，因为它在训练时需要对每个4-bit权重进行反量化操作，导致比fp16 LoRA训练更慢。作者希望找到一种方法在不牺牲精度和内存优势的情况下，回收部分训练时间。

Method: 提出AQLoRA配方：（1）对权重执行单次CPU遍历，按NF4重建误差对层排名，在内存预算下将top-K层保留为fp16，跳过反量化以提速；（2）无需搜索和校准数据；（3）提供质量设置（适应每层）和速度设置（仅适应top块，早停反向传播）；（4）该规则可在数秒内复现Unsloth手工设计的动态4-bit选择。

Result: 在Commonsense-170K上评估了六种模型和四种架构家族（1.4B到14B）。速度设置比调优良好的QLoRA快11.1±2.7%（9次独立时序会话中全部更快，最差7%），损失约1个精度点；质量设置快4.8±2.4%，精度上与QLoRA持平且与fp16 LoRA相差1点以内，仅多用0.2GiB内存。控制实验显示按权重密度或量化误差选择适配层无益，速度效果来自受保护层数量而非身份。

Conclusion: AQLoRA通过简单的层排名规则即可在秒级复现需要反复校准的搜索式分配方案的质量，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升QLoRA训练速度。同时论文总结了在共享硬件上进行时序测量必须遵循的三条规则：固定测量时长而非步数、使用重复臂测噪声底而非近似方法、重复完整会话以获取真实的误差估计。

Abstract: Quantized fine-tuning (QLoRA) saves memory but not time. It dequantizes every 4-bit weight on the fly, so it trains more slowly than fp16 LoRA. We present AQLoRA (Adaptive-Quantization LoRA), a recipe that buys part of that time back. One CPU pass over the weights sets everything, with no search and no calibration data. The pass ranks layers by NF4 reconstruction error and keeps the top-K in fp16 under a memory budget. Those layers skip dequantization, which is where the speed comes from. A quality setting adapts every layer. A speed setting adapts only the top blocks, so the backward pass stops early. The rule reproduces Unsloth's hand-curated dynamic-4bit selection exactly, in seconds, where search-based allocation needs repeated calibration passes.
  We evaluate on Commonsense-170K across six models and four architecture families, from 1.4B to 14B. The speed setting trains 11.1 +/- 2.7% faster than well-tuned QLoRA and gives up about one accuracy point. It was faster in all nine independent timing sessions, at worst by 7%. The quality setting trains 4.8 +/- 2.4% faster. Its accuracy is level with QLoRA on every model and within a point of fp16 LoRA, for 0.2 GiB more memory. These error bars are measured between independent sessions, not within one. Earning them taught us three rules for timing on shared hardware. Fix the measurement duration, not the step count. Measure the noise floor from a duplicated arm, not a nearly identical method. Repeat whole sessions: a floor computed inside one sweep understates the real uncertainty several times over, and the random seed controls almost none of it.
  We validate the recipe with controls and report the two that failed. Choosing adapter layers by weight density is no better than random. Choosing protected layers by quantization error is not either. The count of protected layers, not their identity, carries the speed effect.

</details>


### [29] [Robust Data-Collection Policy Learning for Low-Variance Online Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24146)
*Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Licheng Luo,Rohan Chandra,Nan Jiang,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双循环梯度算法，用于学习对转移函数不确定性鲁棒且高效的behavior policy，以降低强化学习策略评估中在线评估方差，并提供了全局收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的on-policy方法在策略评估时往往面临高方差问题。已有的behavior policy search方法虽然能降低在线评估方差，但未考虑转移函数的不确定性。在实际中，仿真器的转移函数常因建模误差而与真实环境存在偏差，导致在仿真中训练的behavior policy在真实环境中部署时仍可能出现高方差，从而依赖昂贵的真实世界评估样本。

Method: 提出一种双循环（double-loop）基于梯度的算法用于学习behavior policy。理论部分推导了新颖的转移方差（transition-variance）梯度表达式，并建立了算法的全局收敛性保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法对转移扰动的敏感性低于已有的方法，证明了其在实际应用中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文的方法能够学习到既高效又对转移不确定性鲁棒的behavior policy，为存在仿真与真实环境差异的场景下降低策略评估方差提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning policy evaluation, classic on-policy methods often suffer from high variance when estimating policy performance. To mitigate this issue, behavior policy search has been proposed to learn data-collecting policies tailored to reduce online evaluation variance. However, these approaches do not account for uncertainties in the transition functions. In practice, simulator transitions often differ from the real world due to modeling errors or approximation limitations. As a result, behavior policies trained in simulation may still yield high variance when deployed in real environments, leading to costly reliance on real-world evaluation samples. In this work, we propose a double-loop gradient-based algorithm for learning behavior policies that are both efficient and robust to transition uncertainty. Theoretically, we derive novel transition-variance gradient expressions and establish global convergence guarantees for the algorithm. Numerically, we demonstrate that our method is less sensitive to transition perturbations than existing approaches, providing supportive evidence for its practical utility.

</details>


### [30] [Generating Intervention Hypotheses using Explainable Explanations on Graphs: G2I, a Two-Stage Greedy Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23835)
*Mulin Tian,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将反事实解释重构为干预设计问题的可扩展方法，通过贪心搜索生成局部反事实并转化为可解释规则，同时将网络级干预选择建模为预算约束下的DNF覆盖问题，并利用其近似子模性提供理论保证。实验表明该方法在合成图和真实自杀风险网络上比基于掩码的反事实方法（如CF-GNNExplainer和CF²）具有显著更高的效率和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生和社会科学中的真实决策需要预测模型，但将预测转化为有效干预要求解释模型行为。现有GNN解释方法大多在节点层面操作，难以支持可操作的网络级干预设计；现有反事实GNN解释器（如CF-GNNExplainer和CF²）依赖连续掩码优化，隐含假设边可操作，可能将努力分配到不可变或不可操作的属性上，计算开销大，且解释结果难以被非AI专家理解。核心问题：简单方法能否生成好的解释？

Method: 将反事实解释重构为干预设计问题。局部层面：通过贪心搜索直接识别节点特征和邻居级条件的最小化、可操作改变，并推导贪心搜索提供保证的条件（经验上近似满足），再将反事实转化为适合实际干预的可解释规则。网络层面：将干预选择形式化为预算约束下的析取范式（DNF）覆盖问题，证明目标函数非递减且近似子模，从而可用贪心算法获得理论保证。

Result: 在合成图和真实自杀风险网络上的实验表明，该方法能产生可扩展、成本有效的干预策略，相比基于掩码的反事实方法（CF-GNNExplainer和CF²）实现了显著更高的效率提升。

Conclusion: 简单方法可以生成高质量的解释。通过将反事实解释重新定义为干预设计问题，贪心搜索与DNF覆盖相结合可提供高效、可扩展且具有理论保证的解释与干预策略，优于复杂的掩码优化方法，且更易于被领域专家理解。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making in public health and social science can greatly benefit from predictive models, yet translating predictions into effective interventions requires explaining the model behavior. While Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are well-suited for modeling relational data, existing explanation methods largely operate at the node level and fall short of supporting actionable, network-level intervention design. Existing counterfactual GNN explainers, such as CF-GNNExplainer and CF$^2$, rely on continuous mask optimization over features and edges, which implicitly assume feasible edge manipulation, may allocate effort to immutable or non-actionable attributes, and incur substantial computational overhead. Further, the method of arriving at the explanation itself is difficult to explain to a domain specialist who is not an AI expert. Can simple methods generate good explanations? To explore this, we reframe counterfactual explanation as an intervention design problem. At the local level, we generate counterfactuals via a greedy search that directly identifies minimal, actionable changes to node features and neighbor-level conditions. We derive conditions under which the greedy search provides guarantees, and empirically show that these conditions are approximately met. These counterfactuals are converted into interpretable rules suitable for real-world intervention. At the network level, we formulate intervention selection as a Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) coverage problem under a budget constraint, which is nondecreasing and approximately submodular, enabling a greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world suicide risk networks demonstrate that our approach produces scalable, cost-effective intervention strategies with significantly improved efficiency over mask-based counterfactual methods.

</details>

*Zizhong Wang,Jieying Wang,Zhao Zhang,Jiajia Li*

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Long-context inference in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly limited by the memory required for the key-value (KV) cache. KV cache compression addresses this problem by reducing the storage cost of previous tokens. Among existing approaches, low-rank compression is particularly attractive because it represents every token in reduced dimensions. Previous low-rank methods typically derive fixed projection spaces from model weights, construct fixed spaces from calibration activations, or construct a shared basis over a broad cache region. Such representations may not capture detailed but important information. We partition each per-head KV cache into fixed-length logical pages and observe substantial low-rank structure within individual pages. Based on this observation, we propose PuzzleKV, a training- and calibration-free method that treats each completed page as an independent compression unit. PuzzleKV decomposes pages within each layer and KV head, computes attention directly over dense and factorized pages, and incrementally compresses newly eligible pages during autoregressive decoding. Experiments across models, context lengths, and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of PuzzleKV under matched storage budgets. At approximately 60% of the original KV cache storage, PuzzleKV achieves more than 96% of Full KV performance across both evaluated models and all benchmark settings, with substantial gains over Global SVD on RULER and competitive performance on LongBench. To achieve a more aggressive compression ratio, PuzzleKV can be further combined with quantization while retaining more than 93% of Full KV performance using only 18.7% of the original storage.

</details>


### [32] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在在线线性优化(OLO)和在线凸优化(OCO)中彻底解决了交替后悔(minimax-optimal alternating regret)问题。对概率单纯形上的OLO给出O(log d)常数交替后悔,对一般OCO给出O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔,并均匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替遗憾是与博弈中交替学习动力学相关的一种遗憾概念,用于衡量交替更新策略时的累积损失差。此前结果要么退化(如O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})),要么存在额外log T因子,阻碍了博弈均衡的O(1/T)收敛速率。本文旨在彻底解决该问题的极小极大最优后悔界。

Method: 针对OLO在概率单纯形上的情形,提出一种交替遗憾为O(log d)且与时间T无关的常数后悔算法,并给出匹配下界;针对一般OCO在d维紧凸集上的情形,给出O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔算法,并证明Ω(d log(1+T/d))的匹配下界。

Result: OLO:O(log d)常数交替后悔,显著改善此前O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})结果;由此获得两人零和博弈中O(log d/T)的Nash均衡收敛,以及两人一般和博弈中O(log d/T)的粗相关均衡(CCE)收敛——这是首个无耦合动力学在一般和博弈中达到O(1/T)CCE收敛率的结果。OCO:O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔,优于此前Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3}),并给出匹配下界证明Ω(log T)因子不可消除。

Conclusion: 本文在OLO和OCO两种设定下均取得了极小极大最优的交替后悔界,消除了此前结果中依赖T的多余因子,并首次在两人一般和博弈中实现无耦合学习动力学的O(1/T)CCE收敛速率,具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>

*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Naoshin Anzum Hridi,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal*
