Method: 采用监督集成框架：在小型领域特定数据集上训练分类器，组合异构UQ评分器输出，用于开卷（closed-book）环境下的幻觉检测。跨4个LLM、9个数据集、3种生成模式（短问答、长文本生成、代码生成）进行鲁棒性分析，考察三个维度：样本效率、域内数据集迁移、生成场景依赖性。

Result: 监督集成在32个设置中的30个优于最佳单一评分器，从仅100个标注样本即可获得增益。在域内迁移下分布偏移时仍保持优势，在28个迁移设置中的23个优于最佳非集成评分器。基于采样的黑盒集成几乎与完整集成同样有效，而单次生成的白盒集成收益有限。

Conclusion: 监督集成方法在不同场景下表现出较强的鲁棒性和样本效率，尤其以采样式黑盒集成为代表性的实用方案，能在无需检索和参考文档的条件下可靠地提升幻觉检测性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods are widely used for hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) in closed-book settings where ground-truth evidence is unavailable at inference time. Prior work has proposed combining UQ signals via learned ensembles, but empirical investigations into the robustness of these ensembles are limited. We study a supervised ensembling framework that trains a classifier over heterogeneous UQ-based scorer outputs on a small, domain-specific dataset of labeled LLM responses, then applies it to out-of-sample hallucination classification without retrieval, tools, or reference documents. Across four LLMs, nine datasets, and three generation regimes (short-form QA, long-form generation, and code generation), we provide a systematic robustness analysis along three axes: sample efficiency, in-domain dataset transfer, and generation regime dependence. We find that supervised ensembles outperform the best individual scorer in 30 of 32 settings, with gains realized from as few as 100 labeled instances. Ensembles retain most of their advantage in cases of in-domain transfer under distribution shift, outperforming the best non-ensemble scorer in 23 of 28 transfer settings. Sampling-based black-box ensembles are nearly as effective as full ensembles, while single-generation white-box ensembles offer limited benefit.

</details>


### [68] [It depends: Incorporating correlations for joint aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties of high-dimensional output spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24518)
*Leonhard F. Feiner,Manuel Nickel,Martin Menten,Laurin Lux,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis,Raphael Rehms,Johannes Paetzold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在高维回归任务中联合建模偶然不确定性与认知不确定性的方法，通过低秩加对角协方差结构近似联合不确定性，避免全协方差矩阵的计算负担，并在图像修复、着色、光流和深度估计等任务中取得优异的不确定性量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维回归任务（如医学图像分割、修复）中，偶然不确定性和认知不确定性分别捕捉数据固有噪声和模型在陌生条件下的置信度，建模两者能提升预测的可靠性与透明度。然而现有工作往往只建模其中一种不确定性，或无法高效联合建模两种不确定性，限制了鲁棒性。

Method: 提出将联合不确定性近似为低秩加对角协方差结构，显式地将偶然与认知不确定性整合进统一的二阶分布中，支持采样和对数似然评估等下游分析；同时引入稳定化策略以提高训练和推理效率。

Result: 在图像修复、着色、光流和深度估计等任务上，所提出的方法在不确定性量化方面优于现有方法，证明了联合建模两种不确定性并采用低秩加对角结构的有效性。

Conclusion: 低秩加对角协方差结构能够在可承受的计算成本下高效地联合建模偶然与认知不确定性，为高维回归任务提供更可靠且透明的不确定性估计，并支持丰富的下游分析。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) plays a vital role in enhancing the reliability of deep learning model predictions, especially in scenarios with high-dimensional output spaces. This paper addresses the dual nature of uncertainty -- aleatoric and epistemic -- focusing on their joint integration in high-dimensional regression tasks. For example, in applications like medical image segmentation or restoration, aleatoric uncertainty captures inherent data noise, while epistemic uncertainty quantifies the model's confidence in unfamiliar conditions. Modeling both jointly enables more reliable predictions by reflecting both unavoidable variability and knowledge gaps, whereas modeling only one limits transparency and robustness. We propose a novel approach that approximates the resulting joint uncertainty using a low-rank plus diagonal covariance structure, capturing essential output correlations while avoiding the computational burdens of full covariance matrices. Unlike prior work, our method explicitly combines aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties into a unified second-order distribution that supports robust downstream analyses like sampling and log-likelihood evaluation. We further introduce stabilization strategies for efficient training and inference, achieving superior UQ in the tasks of image inpainting, colorization, optical flow, and depth estimation.

</details>


### [69] [From Numerical Simulators of PDEs to Neural Emulators and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24547)
*Felix Koehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经仿真器与传统PDE数值求解器并非对立，而是相辅相成。通过模式级傅里叶分析统一审视求解器误差、架构归纳偏置与训练目标，论文贡献了三项成果：APEBench基准测试套件、渐进细化可微物理研究以及神经仿真器优越性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE数值求解器计算成本高昂，尤其在需要快速或大量查询评估的场景成为瓶颈。神经仿真器虽然能带来显著加速，但通常被视为产生其训练数据的传统方法的黑箱替代品，二者之间的深层联系未被充分探索。

Method: 论文将求解器在仿真器学习流程中的多重角色进行解耦，采用模式级（mode-wise）傅里叶分析作为统一语言，同时刻画求解器误差、架构归纳偏置与训练目标。在此基础上构建三项工作：(1) APEBench——基于JAX中快速可微伪谱求解器的自回归神经仿真器综合基准套件；(2) 渐进细化可微物理——研究未收敛求解器对代理模型训练的影响；(3) 神经仿真器优越性——分析数值误差与架构归纳偏置的影响。

Result: 论文通过统一的分析框架表明神经架构与传统离散化方法在结构上相互映射，其误差同样适用于谱分析。三项贡献分别提供了基准测试工具、对未收敛求解器训练效应的深入理解，以及对数值误差与归纳偏置共同作用的系统性分析。

Conclusion: 神经仿真器与传统求解器两大范式本质上比想象的更为相似，二者之间的洞察可以双向流动。通过解耦求解器的多重角色并借助傅里叶分析这一共同语言，可以更深入地理解神经仿真器的行为，并实现更有原则性的设计与训练。

Abstract: Simulation is central to modern engineering and science, but the cost of numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a bottleneck whenever fast or many-query evaluations are required. Neural emulators trained on solver-generated data promise significant speedups, yet they are usually framed as opaque alternatives to the very methods that produce their training signal. This thesis argues the two paradigms are more alike than different: neural architectures mirror classical discretizations, their errors are amenable to the same spectral analysis, and insight flows profitably in both directions. We approach the relationship by disentangling the multiple roles a solver plays in the emulator learning pipeline. Mode-wise Fourier analysis then provides a common language in which solver errors, architectural inductive biases, and training objectives can all be read off simultaneously. Taken together, this allows synthesizing three contributions. (1) APEBench, a comprehensive benchmarking suite for autoregressive neural emulators of PDEs that uses fast differentiable pseudo-spectral solvers in JAX. (2) Progressively Refined Differentiable Physics, an investigation of the effect of unconverged solvers on surrogate training. (3) Neural Emulator Superiority, an analysis of the influence of numerical errors and architectural inductive biases.

</details>


### [70] [Persistent Cross Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24549)
*Sijin Yeom,Jae-Hun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持久交叉熵（PCE），通过对齐两个持久图的概率空间，将传统交叉熵扩展到持久图领域。通过相似度函数与持久性权重结合构建诱导概率，弥补了两个持久图事件空间不一致的问题。论文证明了诱导概率和PCE的主要性质及稳定性定理，并通过三个数值实验展示了PCE在区分同持久熵图、识别动力系统因果方向以及作为知识蒸馏的方向性拓扑损失方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久熵是持久图上基于持久性概率测度的香农熵，但其交叉熵版本无法自然定义，因为两个持久图通常具有不同的事件空间。这使得将交叉熵这一经典信息论工具应用于持久图分析面临根本性障碍。

Method: 将相似度函数与持久性权重相结合来定义诱导概率，将其中一个图的信息映射到另一个图的事件空间上，未解释的概率质量被分配给未解释事件。基于诱导概率将交叉熵扩展到持久图，形成持久交叉熵（PCE）。

Result: 证明了诱导概率和PCE的主要性质及两者的稳定性定理。三个数值实验表明：PCE能区分具有相同持久熵的图；无需构建联合持久图即可分离动力系统中的因果方向；可作为知识蒸馏中的方向性拓扑损失。

Conclusion: 持久交叉熵为持久图提供了有理论保证的交叉熵定义，不仅弥补了持久熵无法进行图间比较的缺陷，还在因果推断和知识蒸馏等实际任务中展现出应用价值。

Abstract: Persistent entropy is the Shannon entropy of a persistence-based probability measure defined on a persistence diagram. However, its cross-entropy version is not naturally defined because two persistence diagrams generally have different event spaces. To bridge these event spaces, we combine a similarity function with persistence weighting to define an induced probability. The induced probability reflects information from one diagram on the event space of the other diagram and assigns unexplained probability mass to the unexplained event. Using the induced probability, we extend cross entropy to persistence diagrams, called persistent cross entropy (PCE). We establish the main properties of both the induced probability and PCE and prove stability theorems for both. Through three numerical studies, we show that PCE distinguishes diagrams with the same persistent entropy, separates causal directions in dynamical systems without constructing a joint persistent diagram, and can be used as a directional topology loss for knowledge distillation.

</details>


### [71] [FraudBench: Protocol-Sensitive Benchmarking of Adversarial Robustness for Financial Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24551)
*Xitong Zeng,Zhaoge Bi,Yitian Yang,Huaming Chen,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FraudBench，一个协议敏感的对抗鲁棒性基准，用于金融欺诈和信用风险检测。通过三种匹配的评估协议（无约束攻击、事后可行性过滤、部署感知的约束集成攻击）对比，证明鲁棒性结论高度依赖评估协议，且应将领域约束集成到攻击生成中而非作为事后检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融表格数据中的对抗鲁棒性评估面临领域特定约束、严重类别不平衡和攻击者能力不对称等挑战。作者认为鲁棒性不仅是模型的属性，也是评估协议的属性——不同约束和能力的施加方式会导致截然不同的鲁棒性结论，因此需要协议敏感的基准来系统研究这一问题。

Method: 构建FraudBench基准，在相同数据集-模型-攻击-防御设置下比较三种匹配的评估协议：(1)无约束攻击；(2)事后可行性过滤；(3)部署感知的约束集成攻击。覆盖四个公开金融数据集，评估神经网络、树模型和集成模型，使用三种攻击设置（包括白盒和黑盒）。

Result: 关键结果：在Lending Club贷款数据白盒设置下，事后过滤平均仅留下3.7个可行翻转样本，而攻击内投影配合攻击者可变性掩码在相同扰动预算下产生2,832.3个可行翻转样本。IEEE-CIS数据集显示可行性和攻击者能力是独立的轴；黑盒评估表明协议选择可以改变模型家族的排名。

Conclusion: 金融欺诈鲁棒性评估应同时报告预测性能下降和攻击可行性，且领域约束应被集成到攻击生成过程中，而非作为事后后处理检查。协议选择本身会显著影响鲁棒性评估结论。

Abstract: Machine learning models are widely used in financial fraud and credit-risk detection, yet their adversarial robustness remains difficult to evaluate because financial tabular data involve domain-specific constraints, severe class imbalance, and asymmetric attacker capability. We argue that, in this setting, robustness is not only an attribute of the model, but also an attribute of the evaluation protocol. Different ways of enforcing constraints and capability can lead to substantially different robustness conclusions. This paper presents FraudBench, a protocol-sensitive benchmark for adversarial robustness evaluation in financial fraud and credit-risk detection. Rather than treating domain constraints as post-hoc validity checks, FraudBench evaluates the same dataset--model--attack--defence setting under three matched protocols: unconstrained attacks, post-hoc feasibility filtering, and deployment-aware constraint-integrated attacks. FraudBench covers four public financial datasets, and evaluates neural, tree-based, and ensemble models using three attack settings. Our results show that robustness conclusions are highly protocol-sensitive. On Lending Club Loan Data under the white-box setting, post-hoc filtering leaves only 3.7 feasible-flipped examples on average, whereas in-attack projection with attacker mutability masking produces 2,832.3 feasible-flipped examples under the same perturbation budget. The results on IEEE-CIS further show that feasibility and attacker capability are separate axes, while black-box evaluation shows that protocol choice can alter model-family rankings. These findings suggest that fraud robustness evaluation should report predictive degradation and attack feasibility jointly, and should incorporate domain constraints into attack generation rather than treating them as post-processing checks.

</details>


### [72] [SeisMamba: Low-Latency Single-Station Seismic Magnitude Estimation for Spatially Distributed Earthquake Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24561)
*Quenton Yeo,Zhaoge Bi,Linghan Huang,Luke Stephen Higgins,Flora Salim,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeisMamba，一种基于Mamba（选择性状态空间模型）的轻量级单站地震震级快速估计架构。该模型在STEAD基准上取得最优MSE、RMSE和R²指标，推理速度比基于Transformer的基线快约3倍（0.55ms/32波形），并在智利-台湾跨区域留出实验中保持有效性能，为低成本地震早期预警提供了精度-延迟兼顾的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震早期预警系统依赖密集区域台网和区域特定标定，导致高地震风险但传感基础设施稀疏的区域存在空间覆盖壁垒。单站学习方法虽有成本优势，但现有模型常面临精度与延迟的权衡，且在区域分布偏移下性能退化。因此需要一种轻量、低延迟且具跨区域泛化能力的单站震级估计模型。

Method: SeisMamba采用Mamba（稀疏选择性状态空间模型）作为核心架构，集成四个关键组件：分层卷积编码（hierarchical convolutional encoding）用于特征提取；稀疏选择性状态空间建模用于高效长序列波形分析；多尺度特征融合（multi-scale feature fusion）；以及辅助时间预测头（auxiliary temporal prediction head）来增强时序建模能力。模型输入为单站未经过度预处理的三分量地震波形。

Result: 在STEAD基准上，SeisMamba在MSE、RMSE和R²三项指标上均优于所有测试基线；在NVIDIA T4 GPU上对32个波形批次仅需0.55ms推理时间，比基于Transformer的基线快约3倍。在智利-台湾跨区域hold-out实验中，模型在地理未见过的地震区域上保持了有效性能。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间波形建模为空间分布广泛、低成本的地震早期预警提供了一个有前景的精度-延迟骨干架构，能够缓解传统区域依赖模型的覆盖壁垒问题。

Abstract: Rapid earthquake magnitude estimation is central to earthquake early warning, yet many operational systems depend on dense regional seismic networks and region-specific calibration. This creates a spatial coverage barrier for high-risk areas with sparse sensing infrastructure. Single-station learning offers a lower-cost alternative, but existing models often face an accuracy--latency trade-off and may degrade under regional distribution shift. We present SeisMamba, a lightweight Mamba-based architecture for low-latency magnitude estimation from minimally processed three-component seismic waveforms recorded at a single station. SeisMamba combines hierarchical convolutional encoding, sparse selective state-space modelling, multi-scale feature fusion, and an auxiliary temporal prediction head to support efficient long-sequence waveform analysis. On the STEAD benchmark, SeisMamba achieves the best MSE, RMSE, and $R^2$ among tested baselines while requiring only 0.55 ms for a batch of 32 waveforms on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it about three times faster than transformer-based baselines. We further conduct a Chile--Taiwan regional hold-out experiment as a diagnostic test of cross-region deployment, where SeisMamba retains useful performance on geographically unseen seismic regions. These results suggest that selective state-space waveform modelling provides a promising accuracy--latency backbone for spatially distributed, low-cost earthquake early warning.

</details>


### [73] [Across the Loss Landscape with Progressive Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24568)
*Paul Caillon,Christophe Cerisara,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度神经网络中渐进式"生长-再优化"策略如何将训练过程偏向更平坦的极小值区域。作者将生长视为渐进式约束松弛：从低维子模型出发，通过解锁嵌套的随机子空间逐步扩展可训练参数，同时冻结正交补空间，并在每次扩展后重新优化。在标准的局部正则条件下，作者证明了局部子水平集可由椭球近似，且冻结约束下的盆地可达性可由冻结方向的显式有效曲率刻画。该理论解释了渐进式生长通过冻结约束引起的体积效应增大宽盆地相对权重、抑制窄盆地。作者在玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100中验证了该预测，发现渐进式子空间生长确实产生更平坦的解，但曲率降低并不普遍转化为测试性能提升，揭示了平坦度与泛化之间关系的微妙性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在高度非凸、过参数化的损失景观中仍能良好泛化，这一现象常与随机优化所找到的极小值的几何性质相关。作者希望从理论上理解渐进式"生长并优化"策略（如渐进式训练、逐层解锁等）如何系统性地将优化偏向更平坦的区域，从而揭示这类策略的偏差机制。

Method: 作者将增长过程建模为渐进式约束松弛：从低维子模型开始，迭代地解锁嵌套的随机子空间并冻结其正交补空间（保持在网络初始化值），每次解锁后重新优化。在非退化极小值周围的局部正则性假设下，作者给出两个理论结果：(1) 局部子水平集可由椭球良好近似；(2) 冻结约束下的盆地可达性可由冻结方向的有效曲率显式刻画。由此论证渐进式增长通过体积效应增大宽盆地权重、抑制尖盆地。随后在玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100上实证验证该理论。

Result: 实验证实渐进式子空间增长确实可靠地产生更平坦的解，与理论预测一致。然而，曲率的降低并不普遍地转化为测试集性能的提升，这表明平坦度与泛化能力之间的关系存在微妙性——平坦不一定代表更好的泛化。

Conclusion: 渐进式增长策略通过冻结约束引发的体积效应对损失景观中的盆地产生选择性偏差，倾向于宽盆地而抑制尖盆地，这为理解生长类策略的隐式正则化提供了理论基础。但平坦度与泛化的因果关系需要更细致的审视，不能简单地将平坦性等同于泛化性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks generalize well despite their highly nonconvex, overparameterized loss landscapes, a phenomenon often associated with the geometry of the minima found by stochastic optimization. We study how incremental grow-and-optimize strategies bias training toward flatter regions by viewing growth as progressive constraint relaxation. Starting from a low-dimensional submodel, we iteratively expand the trainable parameters by unlocking nested random subspaces while freezing the orthogonal complement at the network initialization, re-optimizing after each expansion until the full architecture is reached. Under standard local regularity conditions around non-degenerate minima, we prove that local sublevel sets are well approximated by ellipsoids and that basin accessibility under frozen constraints can be characterized by an explicit effective curvature in the frozen directions. This leads to an explanation of the bias: progressive growth increases the relative weight of wide basins and suppresses sharp ones through a volume effect induced by the frozen constraints. We empirically validate these predictions in controlled toy landscapes and in a realistic ResNet/CIFAR-100 setting and confirm that although progressive subspace growth reliably produces flatter solutions, curvature reductions do not universally translate into improved test performance, highlighting subtleties in the flatness-generalization connection. The code is available at https://github.com/p0lcAi/Across-the-Loss-Landscape.

</details>


### [74] [IAPO: Influence-Aware Policy Optimization for Credit Assignment in Multi-Turn Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24588)
*Bo Ren,Yirong Mao,Yi Yang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出影响感知策略优化（IAPO），通过将每个轨迹表示为带类型的依赖图，利用用户和工具观察作为证据，将轨迹级优势重分配为路由权重，从而在稀疏结果反馈下改进多轮服务智能体的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多轮交互中，任务信息随时间逐步展开而非初始即完整指定，最终奖励无法指示哪些动作真正有助于任务解决。现有方法依赖跨轨迹对比证据或单独构建的步骤级学习信号来细化信用分配，但一个已完成的展开过程本身已记录了信息与错误如何在智能体动作之间流动，这一内部证据未被充分利用。

Method: IAPO将每条轨迹表示为带类型的"影响依赖图"（influence-dependency graph），覆盖可训练的智能体动作节点，用户与工具观察作为证据。该方法将支持使用（support-use）和失败使用（failed-use）的结构转换为路由权重，用于重新分配同一轨迹级优势值，实现无需额外轨迹或单独步骤信号的信用细化。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，IAPO在三个服务智能体基准（τ²-Bench、UserBench、AgentChangeBench）上超越多轮强化学习基线；BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn进一步表明这些增益不会损害多轮函数调用性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作推进了对多轮用户交互中信用分配的理解，为从稀疏结果反馈训练服务智能体提供了一种有原则的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-turn interactions with users and external tools. In these settings, relevant task information often unfolds over time rather than being fully specified at the initial prompt. Service agents make this challenge especially concrete: users may clarify or revise their goals, while tool responses provide information needed for subsequent decisions. Thus, a final reward alone cannot indicate which actions contributed to resolving the task. Recent methods rely on comparative evidence from other trajectories or resampled continuations, or on separately constructed step-level learning signals, to refine credit. However, a completed rollout already records how information and errors flow between agent actions. We introduce Influence-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), which represents each rollout as a typed influence-dependency graph over trainable agent actions, with user and tool observations serving as evidence. IAPO converts support-use and failed-use structure into routing weights that redistribute the same trajectory-level advantage. Experiments with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B demonstrate superior performance over multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) baselines across three service-agent benchmarks: {τ^2}-Bench, UserBench, and AgentChangeBench. BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn further shows that these gains do not compromise multi-turn function-calling performance. This work advances the understanding of credit assignment in multi-turn user interactions and provides a principled approach to training service agents from sparse outcome feedback.

</details>


### [75] [Taming foundation model with invariance-oriented pre-training for broad-spectrum EEG analysis across signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24597)
*Yulong Dou,Han Wu,Guo Chen,Fangmao Ju,Zhiming Cui,Dinggang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INCEPT是一个面向EEG的、基于不变性学习的预训练基础模型，在超过11,000小时的无标注临床脑电数据上训练。与传统的重构中心方法不同，INCEPT通过学习相关EEG观测之间的表征级稳定性，分离稳定的神经结构与受试者敏感信息，抑制头皮记录中的噪声变异，同时保留受试者、状态和条件判别信息。在涵盖信号级评估、脑状态解码和脑健康评估三个层次的十数据集基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探针指标中的26个和30个微调指标中的24个排名第一，超越现有的EEG基础模型和任务专用编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数EEG模型仍局限于"一数据集一模型"的有监督范式，而现有的EEG基础模型多为重构中心，假设可从局部上下文预测的EEG内容必然是迁移性神经信息。这种假设忽略了头皮记录中大量与神经信号无关的噪声变异，限制了表征的迁移性和通用性。因此，作者提出以不变性学习为原则构建可复用的EEG基础模型。

Method: INCEPT采用不变性导向的预训练策略，而非仅依赖信号重建。其核心思想是在相关的EEG观测对之间学习表征级稳定性，将稳定的神经结构与受试者敏感信息与主导头皮记录的扰动变异分离开来，同时保留受试者、状态和条件可判别的信息。模型在超过11,000小时的无标注临床EEG数据上进行预训练。

Result: 在十个数据集、三个分析层次（信号级评估、脑状态解码、脑健康评估）的基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探针指标中的26个和30个微调指标中的24个排名第一，优于现有的EEG基础模型，并超越了多个下游场景中的任务专用编码器。消融实验和表征分析表明，不变性导向的预训练相比纯重构方法能更好地提升迁移性能并组织受试者敏感的神经表征。

Conclusion: 不变性学习被证明是构建可复用EEG基础模型的一个有前景的原则。通过在学习目标中强调表征层面的跨观测稳定性而非仅仅信号重建，可以显著提升模型的迁移能力和泛化表现，为大规模EEG基础模型的设计提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used window into human brain function, but most EEG models remain tied to a one-dataset-one-model supervised paradigm. Recent EEG foundation models offer a route toward reusable representations, but most remain reconstruction-centered, assuming that EEG content predictable from local context is necessarily transferable neural information. Here we present INCEPT, an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabelled clinical EEG. Rather than prioritizing signal recovery alone, INCEPT learns representation-level stability across correlated EEG observations, separating stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive information from the nuisance variability that dominates scalp recordings while preserving subject-, state- and condition-discriminative information. We evaluate INCEPT on a broad-spectrum benchmark of ten datasets spanning three levels of post-acquisition EEG analysis: signal-level assessment, brain-state decoding, and brain-health evaluation. INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 of 30 fine-tuning metrics, and also surpasses strong task-specific specialist encoders across diverse downstream settings. Objective ablations and representation analyses further show that invariance-oriented pre-training improves transfer and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations beyond reconstruction alone. These results establish invariance learning as a promising principle for building reusable EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [76] [Conditional GraphGANFed: Optimizing Graph-Structured Molecule Generation in Federated Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24610)
*Daniel Manu,Abee Alazzwi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cGraphGANFed，在GraphGANFed框架中引入critic网络，利用用户定义指标评估生成的分子，并将critic网络和判别器的评估结果整合到生成器损失函数中，以生成既保持化学性质相似又优化用户指定指标的分子。实验表明cGraphGANFed在Validity、LogP和QED等指标上显著优于GraphGANFed，并增强了应对模式坍塌和非独立同分布数据的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphGANFed虽然通过联邦学习和图卷积网络实现了隐私保护下的GAN分子生成，但其无法生成仅优化用户定义指标的合成分子，这在药物发现过程中是一个关键需求，因此需要扩展以支持条件化分子生成。

Method: 在GraphGANFed基础上引入critic网络评估生成分子是否符合用户定义指标，将critic网络输出与判别器的评估结果共同整合进生成器的损失函数中，从而引导生成器在保持化学性质的同时优化指定指标。

Result: 在两种场景下验证：（1）优化全部七种常用指标时，cGraphGANFed在Validity和LogP上显著优于GraphGANFed，在QED上略有优势；（2）仅优化QED时，cGraphGANFed生成的分子QED提升超过10%。此外，cGraphGANFed对模式坍塌和非IID数据导致的性能下降具有更强的韧性。

Conclusion: cGraphGANFed通过critic网络实现了用户约束下的条件分子生成，在保持数据隐私的同时显著提升了分子质量指标，并对联邦学习中常见的非IID数据等问题具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have garnered considerable attention in molecular discovery for their ability to generate novel and high-quality molecules. To efficiently train a GAN model while preserving data privacy, GraphGANFed has been proposed to incorporate federated learning and graph convolutional networks into GAN. Yet, GraphGANFed cannot produce synthetic molecules that only optimize a user-defined metric(s) to facilitate the new drug discovery process. To address this issue, we introduce a novel extension to GraphGANFed, namely conditional GraphGANFed (cGraphGANFed), by incorporating the critic network to assess generated molecules using user-defined metric(s). The evaluation results from both the critic network and discriminator are integrated into the loss function of the generator, guiding it to generate novel molecules that maintain similar chemical properties to real ones while optimizing user-defined metrics. Extensive simulations are conducted in two scenarios. First, cGraphGANFed endeavors to optimize all seven commonly used metrics, and the results show that cGraphGANFed significantly outperforms GraphGANFed in Validity and LogP, with a slight advantage in QED, across different settings. Second, cGraphGANFed focuses solely on optimizing QED, and the results show that the synthetic molecules produced by cGraphGANFed can achieve more than 10% improvement in QED than GraphGANFed. Also, the results demonstrate cGraphGANFed has enhanced resilience against mode collapses and performance reduction caused by non-IID data.

</details>


### [77] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在拟阵约束下对抗性bandit子模函数最大化的在线学习问题，首次提出了一个具有次线性遗憾界的随机oracle-多项式时间算法，其期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏在一般拟阵约束下对抗性bandit子模最大化的次线性遗憾算法。该问题在在线推荐、组合优化等场景中有重要应用，但直接处理指数多个交换策略在时间和空间上均不可行，需要新的技术突破。

Method: 本文将问题转化为在Poisson基游走（Poisson base walk）上学习交换策略，从而将问题与上下文bandit问题建立联系并获取信息论层面的次线性遗憾保证。针对直接学习指数多个策略不可行的问题，作者引入了"平衡分数交换"（balanced fractional exchanges）技术，将策略混合压缩为单个分数基，在保留Poisson分析所需交换信息的同时，实现了多项式时间算法。

Result: 提出了第一个在一般拟阵约束下对抗性bandit子模最大化的次线性遗憾算法，每轮只需一次可行值查询，期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，且算法在时间和空间上均为多项式复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作通过连接Poisson基游走、上下文bandit和平衡分数交换技术，解决了对抗性bandit子模最大化在一般拟阵约束下的核心难题，实现了遗憾界与计算复杂度之间的良好平衡，是该研究方向的一个重要突破。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [78] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据上系统比较不同的测试集划分策略（随机划分、留州法、留事件法），揭示了现有电力中断预测模型评估中的严重缺陷：随机划分因空间和时间自相关导致性能被高估，在空间与时间留出实验中模型精度大幅下降甚至不如简单基线，GeoAI基础模型嵌入仅带来有限的、不稳定的改进，无法解决事件级泛化问题。作者认为当前公开训练的停电预测模型在真实部署条件下操作价值有限且不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力中断预测模型越来越多地被用于气候驱动的关键基础设施风险评估中，但现有评估实践未能回答这些模型能否推广到真实部署所需的新场景（新区域、新时间、新事件）。现有评估方法存在系统性盲区，导致模型的泛化能力被严重高估，亟需严谨测试来揭示真实性能。

Method: 采用美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据，构建基于天气再分析数据、土地覆盖特征以及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）嵌入的特征集。系统比较三种测试集选择策略：未过滤的随机划分、留州法（leave-one-state-out，评估空间泛化）和留事件法（leave-one-event-out，评估事件级泛化），并对比各模型在多种评估协议下的预测性能。

Result: 随机训练-测试划分下模型表现强劲，但该结果因空间和时间自相关而被高估；在空间和时间留出实验中，预测精度大幅下降，模型经常无法胜过简单空基线；GeoAI基础模型嵌入带来的改进有限且不一致，主要体现在空间泛化上，无法解决事件级迁移性差的根本问题。

Conclusion: 在现有数据覆盖和评估实践条件下，公开训练的电力中断预测模型在真实部署中操作价值有限且不确定。未来进展需要改进数据覆盖面、采用更贴近现实的评估协议，并将重点从边际模型算法改进转向解决结构性数据约束问题。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [79] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出oFM，一个基于167万真实癌症患者队列开发的基础模型，整合临床轨迹与DNA、RNA及H&E病理等多模态数据，生成患者状态嵌入。在预后基准测试中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期的AUC上优于专家精选特征（总生存期0.774 vs 0.563）；在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM的获益排序AUTOC提升三倍。同时提出基于证据时序图的可解释机制发现框架，连接预测结果与临床和生物学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要能够随时间捕捉癌症演变和治疗过程、整合多模态观测的患者状态的纵向模型，现有方法缺乏对临床轨迹与分子及病理数据的时间整合能力。

Method: oFM基于167万癌症患者的真实世界队列构建，按患者级别划分训练/验证/测试集，超百万患者用于训练。模型对每日临床和分子事件进行编码，并与病理图像时间整合生成患者状态嵌入。此外提出了机制发现框架，通过证据时序图解释下游预测模型结果，将预测与临床和生物学机制关联。

Result: 冻结的oFM嵌入在预后基准上优于专家精选基线特征：治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期的AUC显著提升（总生存期0.774 vs 0.563）。在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入实现三倍更高的合并和尺度归一化治疗获益AUTOC，在9/11队列中获益排序改善，且在双臂内提供更强的预后判别力。

Conclusion: oFM展示了将多模态临床轨迹与分子和病理数据纵向整合构建患者状态嵌入的有效性，在预后预测、治疗获益评估方面显著超越传统专家特征，并通过可解释机制发现框架支持临床和药物开发应用。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [80] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPDVR方法，通过基于轨迹正确性重构采样token OPD的隐含奖励，并应用ReLU门控机制，无缝结合RLVR与on-policy蒸馏，无需额外超参数，在六个推理基准上优于标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR面临稀疏的任务级反馈问题，而OPD提供密集的token级指导但忽略了轨迹正确性且受限于教师模型性能。现有的两者结合方案依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入了额外超参数和权衡。因此需要一个无需额外超参数、能同时利用两者优势的方法。

Method: 首先基于轨迹正确性重构采样token OPD的隐含奖励，然后应用ReLU门控机制：正确轨迹获得非负奖励，错误轨迹获得非正奖励，从而在保持教师分布引导的同时对齐任务成功信号。该修改将采样token OPD转化为一个正规的RLVR方法，可与任意策略梯度算法（如GRPO）无缝结合。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，OPDVR一致优于标准OPD，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OPDVR提供了一种简单且有效的方式无缝结合RLVR和OPD，无需新增超参数，既提供了密集的监督信号又提供了任务级正确性，能稳定提升推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models. However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [81] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种并行化预测编码网络训练方法：通过将生成式预测编码网络（Generative PCN）与支持编码预测编码网络（Encoding PCN）配对训练，使两者并行匹配神经激活，避免传统PCN中顺序反向误差传播的瓶颈，并应用于时间序列异常检测，实现更稳定、连续、在线的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是一种支持神经网络层并行更新的学习范式，但其主要瓶颈在于顺序反向误差传播（sequential backwards error propagation），限制了训练效率和并行能力。

Method: 将生成式预测编码网络（Generative PCN）与支持编码预测编码网络（Encoding PCN）配对，两个PCN并行训练以匹配彼此的神经激活，从而消除了顺序传播的需求，实现并行化训练。

Result: 将该方法应用于时间序列异常检测任务，实验结果表明该方法带来了更稳定、连续、在线的学习表现。

Conclusion: 通过配对两个并行训练的PCN来匹配神经激活，成功解决了PCN中顺序反向传播的瓶颈，为时间序列异常检测提供了更稳定和连续的在线学习方案。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [82] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络中的对称性能否通过参数空间中的运动来实现。作者将该问题形式化为实现映射的提升问题，提出当参数的功能灵敏度张成对称轨道切空间时，存在平滑的参数空间作用；将该条件在最小二乘意义下松弛，可得到两个局部参数方向（跟随对称轨道、朝向等变子空间）。在旋转不变分类器和哈密顿神经网络上的实验表明，这些方向仅产生局部的函数空间运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当一个网络学习到具有已知对称性的函数时，一个重要的问题是这个对称性能否通过参数空间的运动来实现（即参数空间中的群作用能否在函数空间诱导出对应的群作用）。这涉及神经网络可解释性、对称性几何以及参数化的表达能力等核心问题，但目前缺乏系统的形式化表述和验证。

Method: 将对称性移动问题形式化为实现映射Φ:θ→f_θ的提升问题。理论上证明平滑参数空间作用存在的条件为函数对称轨道切空间在dΦ_θ的像（即参数的功能灵敏度）中，且该条件对逐点一阶提升是充分的。在最小二乘意义下松弛该条件得到两个局部参数方向：一个沿对称轨道，一个向等变子空间下降，残差衡量参数化无法到达的部分。

Result: 在旋转不变分类器和具有旋转对称势的哈密顿神经网络上验证了方向的性质，发现这些方向确实诱导期望的函数空间运动，但仅具局部有效性：重新计算的参数方向能跟踪轨道并减少等变缺陷，而固定持有的方向在训练后会偏离轨道。

Conclusion: 参数空间中的对称性移动通常是局部而非全局有效的，参数化本身的几何约束决定了对称性能否被精确实现。该框架为理解网络对称性、参数化表达力以及等变学习提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [83] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线学习场景中的超参数优化问题，提出四种处理边界约束的策略（而非简单的"边界"策略），在基准数据集上验证了所提策略优于传统"boundary"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化对模型性能至关重要，但现有研究多聚焦于批量学习场景，数据流（在线学习）场景下的超参数优化复杂度更高。许多超参数受限于有界搜索空间，优化算子产生的解可能越界，因此需要有效的边界约束处理技术来修正无效解。

Method: 在在线优化算法中提出四种边界约束处理策略，替代传统的"boundary"策略（即简单地将越界值裁剪回边界阈值）。通过在既有基准数据集上进行实证研究，比较所提策略与"boundary"策略的性能。

Result: 在已建立的基准数据集上的实证结果表明，所提出的四种边界策略在性能上优于传统的"boundary"策略。

Conclusion: 在处理有界搜索空间内超参数优化的边界约束问题时，所提出的四种策略相较简单的"boundary"裁剪策略能获得更优的模型性能，为在线学习场景下的自适应超参数优化提供了更有效的约束处理方法。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [84] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架KENDO，通过核集成与自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，并结合不一致感知采集策略，同时适用于贝叶斯优化（KENDO-BO）和贝叶斯主动学习（KENDO-AL），在多目标优化中通过随机标量化扩展，实现高达5倍（BO）和27倍（AL）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中的超参数选择是关键挑战，模型误设定会导致性能次优，而完全贝叶斯处理虽更准确但依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样，需要一种既鲁棒又高效的方法。

Method: 提出统一框架KENDO，用集成高斯过程（EGP）与自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，结合不一致感知（disagreement-aware）采集策略；KENDO-BO和KENDO-AL共享同一自校正机制但采用任务特定的采集目标；多目标扩展通过随机标量化保持单优化器条件结构。

Result: 在单目标、多目标优化和主动学习的合成及真实基准上：KENDO-BO相比最先进方法在优化性能上达到竞争性或更优，计算开销降低最多5倍；KENDO-AL相比基于MCMC的主动学习基线实现更好的预测校准，且加速最多27倍。

Conclusion: KENDO框架证明核集成与不一致感知策略可以统一替代昂贵的MCMC超参数采样，同时提升或保持优化/主动学习性能并大幅降低计算成本，为BO和AL提供了一个高效、鲁棒的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [85] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究参数高效的自我监督适配（仅更新9%参数）能否有效对齐EEG基础模型到目标任务，在三个临床EEG数据集和两个不同预训练目标（对比与掩码重建）的模型上验证，发现该方法显著优于线性探测（AUCPR提升最高20倍），且在固定预算下仅需20-50%无标注数据即可达到峰值性能，性能主要取决于时间窗口多样性而非患者数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练虽承诺可迁移表示，但在多样化临床数据集上泛化能力仍有限；全量微调因计算需求过高不适用于资源受限的临床场景。因此需要探究参数高效的适配策略是否能在极低计算开销下实现对目标任务的有效对齐。

Method: 采用参数高效的自监督适配（SSL adaptation），仅更新9%的模型参数。在两种不同预训练目标的先进模型上验证：BIOT（对比学习）和CBraMod（掩码重建）。在三个临床EEG数据集上进行评估：TUAB（异常检测）、TUEV（事件分类）、CHB-MIT（癫痫检测），并覆盖分布内和分布外两种条件。

Result: SSL适配相比线性探测在AUCPR上获得最高20倍的持续增益；在固定计算预算下，峰值性能仅需20-50%的可用无标注数据；在总时间窗口数固定时，性能对患者数量保持不变，表明性能主要依赖整体时间窗口多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的适配方法能够以极低的计算开销和数据收集负担实现EEG基础模型的有效部署，为资源受限的临床环境提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [86] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为(DNN)²的新方法，将Burer-Monteiro分解应用于Doubly Non-negative (DNN)形式化，用于ReLU神经网络验证。该方法通过一种新颖的特征值最大化过程，在非唯一的对偶乘子空间中搜索有效的全局最优性证书，从而在计算可扩展的同时产生比标准SDP方法更紧的界，且常能匹配精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LP和SDP松弛方法在ReLU神经网络验证中由于松弛间隙过大而给出过度保守的安全保证。虽然CPP形式可以闭合间隙但NP-hard难解，其最便宜的松弛DNN虽然更紧但由于规模超过内点法的处理能力而无法实际应用。关键障碍是DNN中额外的非负性约束导致对偶乘子非唯一，使标准认证方法失效。

Method: 该方法提出一种新颖的特征值最大化程序，通过在非唯一的对偶乘子空间中进行搜索，寻找有效的全局最优性证书。具体而言，将Burer-Monteiro分解应用于DNN形式化，并利用特征值最大化来遍历非唯一乘子空间，以确认全局最优性。

Result: 实验表明，(DNN)²方法产生的边界始终比标准SDP方法更紧，且常能匹配精确解；当有效证书存在时，认证程序能够确认全局最优性。这些结果验证了该方法在紧致性、可认证性和计算可扩展性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作是向提供紧致、可认证且计算可扩展的验证保证迈出的关键一步，有助于在安全攸关的自主系统中部署神经网络控制器和感知模块。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构（factor-space structure）概念，用于描述解耦表征中潜在因子空间的几何特性（如周期性、塌缩、位置相关尺度），并据此提出FactoMap方法，通过将潜在因子的拓扑结构（周期性、塌缩、非均匀范围）迁移到表征中，实现因子连续性与解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦方法通常假设生成因子可用欧几里得乘积坐标表示，但实际因子空间可能存在卷曲、塌缩或位置相关的几何结构。作者指出统计独立的因子不一定几何可分离（如色调与尺度产生不同增长速率的效应，任何固定再缩放都无法消除各向异性），因此需要显式建模因子空间的结构。

Method: 1) 提出因子空间结构的形式化定义，结合因子域（factor domains）、生成器诱导的等价关系（generator-induced identifications）和位置相关尺度来区分拓扑等价但因子几何不同的空间。2) 提出FactoMap（Factor-Space Topographic Map），学习以因子空间格点（lattice）索引的可解释原型，通过拓扑学习将格点的周期性、塌缩和非均匀范围迁移到表征中。

Result: 实验表明，匹配因子空间结构能够保持因子连续性，并实现对潜在因子的有效解耦。

Conclusion: 因子空间结构是解耦表征中不可忽视的几何约束；通过拓扑映射显式保留该结构，可以在经典欧几里得假设失效的场景下仍实现有效的因子解耦。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [88] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LION（Clifford神经范式），基于Clifford代数和解耦图神经范式（传播-聚合）解决多模态属性图中的模态对齐与融合问题。通过构造模态感知几何流形实现模态对齐，并设计自适应全息聚合改善模态融合，在9个文-图MAG数据集上全面超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态领域的快速发展推动图机器学习从文本属性图向多模态属性图（MAG）转变，但现有神经范式存在两大局限：(1) 模态对齐中忽略图上下文：现有方法采用拓扑约束或模态特定算子作为分词器，忽视图上下文并抑制模态交互，导致对齐次优；(2) 模态融合缺乏适应性：多数方法仅是对双模态图的简单适配，未能充分利用具有拓扑先验的对齐token进行融合，导致泛化性差和性能下降。

Method: LION基于Clifford代数和解耦图神经范式（传播-聚合）实现对齐-融合的两阶段流程。首先，构造基于Clifford代数的模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播高效实现模态交互，促进模态对齐。然后，基于对齐token的拓扑感知Clifford分量，提出自适应全息聚合模块，该模块整合逐分量能量和传播尺度信息及可学习参数，以改善模态融合。

Result: 在9个文-图多模态属性图数据集上的大量实验表明，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务中显著优于SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: LION通过Clifford代数驱动的几何流形和自适应全息聚合，有效解决了MAG中模态对齐忽视图上下文及模态融合缺乏适应性的问题，证明了基于几何代数的神经范式在多模态图学习中的优越性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [89] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MDTE框架，一种针对时序图上类别不平衡节点分类问题的少类感知扩散框架，通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定且判别性的时序边事件表示，并引入分布感知选择性传播与多视图判别融合两大模块，在五个真实数据集上显著提升少类性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图上的类别不平衡节点分类面临两大挑战：(1) 多数类主导的时序传播会渐进地同化少数类表示，导致少数类信息被淹没；(2) 传统的节点和邻域信息对少数类缺乏足够的判别性证据，难以进行有效分类。现有方法无法同时解决多数类信息同化和少数类判别信息不足的问题。

Method: MDTE采用条件扩散去噪框架来重建稳定且判别性的时序边事件表示，包含两大核心模块：(1) Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation（分布感知选择性传播），结合基于Local Outlier Factor（LOF）的传播过滤与簇感知低频传播，保留有用的邻域依赖同时抑制有害传播和多数类信息同化；(2) Multi-View Discriminative Fusion（多视图判别融合），利用特征重构与拓扑预测刻画类别间分布学习差异，提取互补的判别性信号以引导扩散去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，MDTE在少数类导向指标上持续取得最佳性能：相对于最强基线，少数类召回率提升最高达23.53个百分点，少数类F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点。

Conclusion: MDTE通过少类感知的扩散框架有效解决了时序图中类别不平衡节点分类的难题，验证了扩散模型在少数类表征学习中的有效性，其两大模块设计（选择性传播与多视图判别融合）为处理多数类信息同化和判别信息不足提供了可行且有效的方案。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型预训练中的有效学习率（ELR）坍缩现象：损失动力学主要由学习率与参数范数的比值（即ELR）决定，而非二者各自独立作用。当ELR在各实验中匹配时，即使学习率和参数范数显著不同，损失轨迹在整个训练过程中也会坍缩到几乎一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型预训练中，学习率和参数范数通常被视为两个独立控制损失动力学的因素，但二者之间的深层关系尚未被阐明。本文旨在探索学习率与参数范数之间是否存在一个更基础的统一坐标，以解释损失动态。

Method: 通过在跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型尺度下进行系统实验，匹配ELR并测量损失轨迹的坍缩误差；同时进行系统性消融以识别影响坍缩精度的关键因素（归一化设计、LR-范数变化时间尺度），并通过受控干预验证权重衰减和超球形（Hyperball）主要通过其诱导的ELR schedule影响损失动力学。进一步将ELR替代LR拟合函数化缩放定律（FSL），并检验其跨范数控制方法的迁移能力。

Result: ELR匹配时损失轨迹坍缩误差通常在10^-3量级，低于代表性配置中种子间变异。权重衰减和Hyperball的损失动态效果主要通过其产生的ELR schedule实现。基于ELR的FSL可跨范数控制方法迁移，并能解释延迟加速这一范数控制的反复出现效应。

Conclusion: ELR是连接学习率调度、范数控制和损失动力学的统一坐标，为理解语言模型预训练动力学提供了共同基准。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [91] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，用于分析基于邻域的公平性审计在有限扰动下的鲁棒性，建立了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化了邻域替换如何传播到审计不稳定，并引入了审计波动性指标来度量公平性审计的预期敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于邻域的公平性审计被广泛使用，但审计过程本身的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。由于这类审计依赖最近邻关系，特征空间中的微小扰动可能改变局部邻域，即使在模型预测不变的情况下也会产生不同的公平性评估结果。

Method: 开发了一个几何框架来分析有界扰动下基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性。具体包括：(1) 建立邻域不变性的充分条件；(2) 量化邻域替换如何传播到审计不稳定；(3) 引入审计波动性指标，度量重复扰动下公平性审计的预期敏感性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验支持了理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释基于邻域的公平性审计所观察到的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为理解基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性提供了理论基础，并通过实验验证了其解释力，有助于更好地理解和评估公平性审计结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [92] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN是一个多模态空间表征学习框架，通过在训练时构建结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性的生物学核，为组织学-转录组学配对学习提供可学习的归纳偏置，从而在保持生物学邻域结构的同时提升比现有方法（如BLEEP）更好的生物学邻域检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组织学-转录组学多模态学习方法过度强调实例级（斑块级）跨模态精确对应，忽略了非配对位点之间共享的分子或空间上下文，导致无法充分保留生物学邻域结构。现有方法缺乏将生物学结构作为显式先验纳入表征学习的机制。

Method: BioKERN在训练阶段构建一个生物学核，该核由转录组相似性和空间邻近性组合而成。该核用于提供分级邻域监督（graded neighborhood supervision），并对嵌入几何进行正则化（regularize embedding geometry），从而将生物学结构作为显式、可学习的归纳偏置引入多模态表征学习。评估采用统一的、与模型无关的生物学邻域定义，并设计了共享架构的对照实验以隔离模型容量与正则化效果的影响。

Result: 在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429两个数据集上，BioKERN在生物学邻域检索任务上相较于BLEEP在单尺度和多尺度设置下均取得了一致的性能提升。受控的共享架构实验表明，大部分性能提升来源于生物学核正则化而非模型容量的增加。

Conclusion: 显式的生物学几何可以作为多模态空间生物学学习中可解释的归纳偏置，有效提升表征对生物学邻域结构的保真度，支持将生物学先验知识作为学习目标的重要组成部分。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [93] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种模型无关的改进方案解决多任务车辆路径规划（VRP）求解器的两大局限：训练端提出POLAR算法，通过本地搜索细化最优解码路径后再构造偏好对，产生更具信息量的成对边际；架构端提出PLE编码器，通过门控机制将共享专家与任务特定专家分层路由，渐进分离通用路径结构与会约束特定的编码。两者结合在16个分布内variant上将平均gap降低21.3%，并在27/32个未见variant上超越先前的神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经多任务VRP求解器面临训练和架构两方面的缺陷：训练上，强化学习因奖励尺度差异和策略改进导致的优势信号萎缩而监督弱化，偏好优化则因采样轨迹近乎相同而受限于策略自身生成解的质量；架构上，全共享编码器会纠缠异构variant之间的约束相关表示，限制泛化能力。

Method: 提出两个模型无关的贡献：(1) POLAR（Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement）训练算法，对最佳解码路径施加局部搜索细化后再构建偏好对，以产生更有效的成对边际；(2) PLE（Progressive Layered Extraction）编码器，每个编码器层通过门控机制路由至一个共享专家和一组任务特定专家，渐进地分离通用路由结构与约束特定编码。

Result: 在16个分布内VRP variant上将平均gap相对最强已发表基线降低21.3%；在32个未见variant中的27个上超越先前神经方法；消融实验证明两个贡献均有效，且跨多种骨干模型架构均能提升跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR与PLE分别从训练信号质量和架构表示两方面有效弥补现有神经多任务VRP求解器的短板，二者叠加可显著提升当前最先进水平，并在跨variant泛化上具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维线性弹性问题的等几何分析在大规模场景下面临计算和存储瓶颈，传统稀疏全网格表示在自由度增长时开销巨大。本文旨在利用等几何分析中基函数的张量积结构以及问题固有的低秩特性，通过张量列（TT）格式来突破这一瓶颈，实现大规模三维弹性模拟的高效可扩展求解。

Method: 将刚度算子、质量算子、力向量和位移解均表示为TT格式；提出两种求解策略：(1) 块算子公式——将耦合弹性算子以分离的TT块存储，引入无矩阵三场TT共轭梯度（CG）求解器；(2) 单算子公式——将完整耦合系统作为单一整体TT算子存储，采用AMEn（交替最小化能量）算法求解。核心在于利用张量积结构基函数和几何相关系数场的低秩性实现压缩表示。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比传统的稀疏全网格表示，TT格式在算子和解上均实现了显著的压缩比，证明了TT公式在大规模三维弹性模拟中具有高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: TT格式为大规模三维等几何弹性分析提供了一种高效、可扩展且存储友好的求解框架。两种求解策略（块算子和单算子）均能有效压缩系统表示，其中块公式结合无矩阵三场TT-CG求解器、单公式结合AMEn算法，共同验证了TT方法应用于三维弹性问题的可行性。

Abstract: This work develops tensor-train (TT) formulations for solving large-scale three-dimensional linear elasticity problems discretized by isogeometric analysis. By exploiting the tensor-product structure of the basis functions and the low-rank structure of geometry-dependent coefficient fields, the stiffness operator, mass operator, force vector, and displacement solution are represented in TT format. Two solution strategies are investigated: a block-operator formulation, in which the coupled elasticity operator is stored as separated TT blocks, and a single-operator formulation, in which the full coupled system is stored as one monolithic TT operator. A matrix-free three-field TT conjugate-gradient solver is introduced for the block formulation, while AMEn is used for the single-operator formulation. Numerical examples demonstrate substantial compression of both operators and solutions compared with conventional sparse full-grid representations, showing that TT-based formulations provide an efficient and scalable approach for large-scale three-dimensional elasticity simulations.

</details>


### [95] [Algorithmic Cost in "Exact Real Computation"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23603)
*Jihoon Hyun,Holger Thies,Martin Ziegler*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了精确实计算的强 Church-Turing 论题：为 ERC 的操作原语分配位成本后，ERC 中多项式开销可计算的实函数与 Turing 机多项式时间可计算的实函数等价，并通过 iRRAM C++ 库中的实现验证了理论性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已在定性层面证明精确实计算（ERC）与 Turing 机（即可计算分析）在表达能力上等价，但尚缺乏定量的多项式时间等价性论证。本文旨在将这种 Turing 完全性从定性强化为定量（强 Church-Turing 论题）。

Method: 为 ERC 的操作原语（包括部分/多值测试）分配位成本（bit-costs），构建一个成本模型，使得任意在 ERC 中产生多项式成本的实函数都能在 Turing 机上以多项式时间计算，反之亦然；并在 iRRAM C++ 库的具体实现上进行运行时测量以验证理论预测。

Result: 建立了 ERC 在多项式时间意义下与 Turing 机（可计算分析）之间的定量等价性，且实现层面的运行时测量与理论性能预测一致。

Conclusion: ERC 不仅定性上等价于 Turing 机，而且在成本分配合理的前提下，满足强 Church-Turing 论题所要求的多项式时间等价性，进一步巩固了 ERC 作为可计算分析实用开发框架的可靠性与可预测性。

Abstract: Turing completeness of a programming language or system characterizes its expressive power; and the strong Church-Turing hypo-/thesis refines such from qualitative to polynomial-time equivalence. Exact Real Computation (ERC) is a novel numerical programming language paradigm: for the imperative processing of continuous data as entities appearing as exact, i.e. devoid of rounding errors [doi:10.1007/978-3-662-44199-2_107]. ERC has been designed [doi:10.46298/lmcs-20(2:17)2024] as convenient and practical alternative, namely proven qualitatively equivalent, to the Turing machines originally underlying Computable Analysis [doi:10.1007/978-3-642-56999-9,doi:10.1007/978-1-4684-6802-1].
  The present work quantitatively strengthens this qualitative Turing-completeness: We assign bit-costs to ERC's operational primitives (including partial/multivalued tests) in such a way that any real function incurring polynomial cost becomes Turing-computable in polynomial time, and vice versa. Runtime measurements on implementations in the iRRAM C++ library confirm our theoretical performance predictions.

</details>


### [96] [Computing statistical Euler limits of the Navier--Stokes equations in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23786)
*Johannes L. Grafen,Tobias Rohner,Stephan Simonis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种蒙特卡洛格子玻尔兹曼方法，用于高效计算三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes与Euler方程的统计解。在粘性消失极限下，该方法通过熵空间自适应松弛高阶动力学矩实现稳定一致的数值解，并完成了三维场景下时间依赖统计解的首次计算，成功恢复K41标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes和Euler方程在湍流无粘极限下存在路径解不唯一、强收敛失效等问题，传统数值方法难以高效计算其统计解。需要一种能够在粘性消失极限下稳定且一致地计算统计解的数值方法。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛格子玻尔兹曼方法（Monte Carlo LBM），结合熵空间-时间自适应松弛（entropic space-time adaptive relaxation）处理高阶动力学矩。在扩散标度下证明离散系综定律沿子序列收敛到Foias-Temam Liouville形式的Navier-Stokes方程解；通过将测度提升到Fjordholm-Mishra-Weber框架，在结构函数标度一致成立时证明无粘极限满足多点统计Euler层次。该方法使用24维初始不确定性的随机Taylor-Green涡进行数值实验。

Result: 该方法实现了三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程沿无粘极限的时间依赖统计解的首次数值计算。数值实验恢复了Kolgomorov K41能量谱和结构函数标度，展示了路径强收敛的失效，与预测的约0.5阶分数1-Wasserstein收敛率一致。基于谱超粘性计算的误差测量，提供了不可压缩Euler方程统计解普适性的数值证据。

Conclusion: 该方法在四个明确假设条件下建立了收敛性结果，无条件建立了弱-强唯一性结论。数值实验验证了理论预测的Wasserstein收敛率，并为Euler统计解的普适性提供了证据，为湍流无粘极限下统计解的数值计算开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: We develop a Monte Carlo lattice Boltzmann method to efficiently compute statistical solutions to the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes and Euler equations. By employing entropic space-time adaptive relaxation for higher order kinetic moments, our scheme is found to yield stable and consistent numerical solutions in the vanishing viscosity limit. We establish a convergence result that is conditional on four explicitly stated assumptions regarding the discrete dynamics. First, under diffusive scaling, the laws of the discrete ensemble are shown to converge along a subsequence to a limit satisfying the Foias--Temam Liouville formulation of the Navier--Stokes equations. Second, by lifting these measures to the Fjordholm--Mishra--Weber framework, we show that, provided the structure-function scaling holds uniformly, the vanishing viscosity limit satisfies the multi-point statistical Euler hierarchy. Unconditionally, we establish weak-strong uniqueness of the limit measures on the interval of existence of a strong Euler solution, and under explicit scaling assumptions, we formally derive a fractional 1-Wasserstein convergence rate of approximately $0.5$. Our method enables the first three-dimensional computations of time-dependent statistical solutions along the inviscid limit of the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Numerical experiments on a randomized Taylor--Green vortex with 24-dimensional initial uncertainty successfully recover Kolmogorov's K41 scaling for energy spectra and structure functions, exhibit the failure of pathwise strong convergence, and are consistent with the predicted Wasserstein convergence rate. Finally, based on error measurements with respect to spectral hyperviscosity computations, we provide numerical evidence for the universality of statistical solutions of the incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [97] [The least squares RBF-PU method for linear elasticity in the diaphragm geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23718)
*Andreas Michael,Elisabeth Larsson,Pierre-Frédéric Villard,Davoud Mirzaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出使用非贴合最小二乘径向基函数分区统一法（unfitted least-squares RBF-PUM）来求解人类膈肌的线性弹性变形问题，该方法特别适合薄几何体的数值模拟，并给出了高阶收敛的理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类膈肌对呼吸至关重要，其数值模拟有助于理解机械通气患者的并发症。但膈肌几何形状极薄，给大多数数值方法带来了巨大挑战，因此需要开发适合薄几何体的高效数值方法。

Method: 采用非贴合最小二乘径向基函数分区统一法（unfitted LS-RBF-PUM），该方法利用非贴合特性和圆柱形片状区域的形状与细化策略，对建模为线弹性模型并施加真实边界条件的真实膈肌几何体进行变形计算。

Result: 推导出理论证明，表明该方法能以高阶收敛到该问题的解。

Conclusion: 非贴合最小二乘径向基函数分区统一法能够有效处理薄几何体上的弹性力学问题，并具备高阶收敛保证，为膈肌等薄组织的数值模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The human diaphragm is vital for respiration and its simulation can aid in further understanding complications arising in individuals that have been subjected to mechanical ventilation. The diaphragm geometry is very thin and hence its numerical simulation poses a challenge for most numerical methods. In this paper, we compute the deformation of real diaphragm geometries which are modelled as linearly elastic and are subject to realistic boundary conditions. The method used is the unfitted least-squares radial basis function partition of unity method, which is well suited to solve problems on thin geometries given its unfitted nature and the shape and refinement of the cylindrical patches that are used. We derive a theoretical proof showing that the method converges with high order to the solution of this problem.

</details>


### [98] [The PWDG-UWVF Equivalence Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23822)
*Shelvean Kapita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从结构上解释了超弱变分公式（UWVF）与标准PWDG通量族中α=β=δ=1/2参数设置之间的等价关系，指出UWVF本质上是破碎场与阻抗迹坐标下的同一边缘耦合问题在不同坐标系的表示，而非仅仅是惩罚参数的特殊选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWVF已知是Trefftz不连续Galerkin（PWDG）方案在特定通量参数下的特例，但此前缺乏对这一事实的结构性解释。作者希望深入阐明UWVF与PWDG之间等价的本质原因，而不仅仅是停留在参数层面的观察。

Method: 作者从每个内网格边上PWDG的两个跳跃项入手，证明在α=β=δ=1/2时，这两项恰好等于UWVF使用的两个定向阻抗-透射缺陷的平方范数之和的一半。由此展示Trefftz-DG范数是全局透射缺陷的自然Riesz范数，其Gram矩阵等于离散矩阵的反厄米部分。进一步通过对离散UWVF应用相同论证，得到互补的正规形式，并揭示了该等价关系本质上是破碎场坐标与阻抗迹坐标之间的转换关系。

Result: 证明在参数α=β=δ=1/2下，PWDG跳跃项与UWVF阻抗-透射缺陷存在精确的范数对应关系；在PWDG坐标中Riesz归一化将算子化为K+iI（K厄米）的正规形式，而直接应用于离散UWVF则得到(1/2)I+iK_U的互补正规形式。

Conclusion: UWVF与PWDG的经典等价性不应仅被视为PWDG代价函数中特定惩罚参数的选择结果，而应理解为同一边缘耦合问题在破碎场坐标与阻抗迹坐标下的两种等价表示，这一视角为两类方法提供了更深刻统一的结构理解。

Abstract: The ultra-weak variational formulation (UWVF) is known to be the Trefftz discontinuous Galerkin scheme obtained from the standard PWDG flux family by setting \(α=β=δ=1/2\). We give a short structural explanation of this fact. On every interior mesh edge, the two PWDG jump terms at these parameter values are exactly one half of the sum of the squared norms of the two oriented impedance-transmission defects used by the UWVF. Thus the Trefftz-DG norm is the natural Riesz norm of the global transmission defect. In PWDG coordinates its Gramian is the anti-Hermitian part of the discrete matrix, and Riesz normalization puts the operator in the normal form \(K+\ii I\), with \(K\) Hermitian. The same argument applied directly to the discrete UWVF yields the complementary normal form \(\tfrac12 I+\ii K_{\rm U}\). This identifies the classical equivalence as a relation between broken-field and impedance-trace coordinates for the same edge-coupling problem, rather than only as a particular choice of penalties.

</details>


### [99] [Randomized Block Davidson Eigensolvers for Plane-Wave Density-Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24529)
*Moritz Gubler,Taejun Park,Augustin Bussy,Johannes Laute,Nicola Marzari,Iurii Timrov,Laura Grigori*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机化块Davidson型特征值求解器，用草图内积空间中的随机化Gram-Schmidt替换传统的欧几里得正交化，仅需单次遍历基向量即可保持条件数有界。该方法在CPU和GPU上以混合精度实现，并集成到DFTK中。在稀疏测试问题上，当矩阵维度超过约2×10^4时性能超越确定性对应方法，在5×10^5维度时快25%；但在完整自洽场DFT计算中，两种Davidson变体相比LOBPCG仅提升5-11%，绘图方案的额外收益有限，其适用性取决于所需状态数随问题规模的增长方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面波密度泛函理论（DFT）中迭代对角化是主要计算成本，其中搜索空间正交化的开销随问题规模和目标态数目增长尤为迅速。传统欧几里得正交化在大规模问题中成为瓶颈，需要设计一种计算效率更高且数值稳定性有保证的特征值求解方法。

Method: 提出随机化块Davidson型特征值求解器：使用草图内积中的随机化Gram-Schmidt替代欧几里得正交化，只需单次遍历基向量；将Rayleigh-Ritz步骤改为确定性的广义Hermitian特征问题；Ritz提取保持精确，保留真Ritz对和交错性质；在CPU/GPU上以混合精度从单一Julia代码实现，与DFTK矩阵无关接口集成，发布为开源RandESC库。

Result: 在稀疏测试问题上（固定特征对数量），草图求解器在矩阵维度约2×10^4时即可超越确定性版本，在5×10^5维度时快25%。在完整自洽场DFT计算中，两种Davidson变体比LOBPCG参考实现总时间快5-11%，但绘图方案的额外收益有限。随着所需状态数随系统规模增长，正交化节省被广义特征问题开销抵消。

Conclusion: 绘图策略的收益区间由所需状态数量随问题维度的缩放关系决定，而非由特征值求解器本身决定。该方法在所需特征对数较少增长的大规模问题中具有明显优势，但当特征对数量随规模快速增长时，绘图带来的正交化节省会被广义特征值问题所抵消。

Abstract: Iterative diagonalization is the dominant cost of plane-wave density-functional theory (DFT), with search-space orthogonalization scaling particularly quickly with problem size and the number of target states. We present a randomized block Davidson-type eigensolver that replaces Euclidean orthogonalization with randomized Gram-Schmidt in a sketched inner product, requiring only a single pass over the basis while keeping its conditioning bounded independently of the input vectors. This modification changes only the Rayleigh-Ritz step, which becomes a definite generalized Hermitian eigenproblem. Ritz extraction remains exact, preserving true Ritz pairs and the interlacing property that makes each band energy an upper bound on the true one. The method is implemented in mixed precision for CPUs and GPUs from a single Julia code, interfaces matrix-free with DFTK, and is released in the open-source RandESC library. On sparse test problems with a fixed number of eigenpairs, the sketched solver overtakes its deterministic counterpart beyond matrix dimensions of about $2\times 10^4$ and is $25\%$ faster at $5\times 10^5$. In full self-consistent field DFT calculations, however, both Davidson variants outperform the locally optimal block preconditioned conjugate gradient (LOBPCG) reference only by $5$ to $11\%$ in total time, while the additional benefit of sketching is limited. As the number of requested states grows with system size, orthogonalization savings are offset by the generalized eigenproblem. Therefore, the regime in which sketching pays off is set by how the number of wanted states scales with the problem dimension, not by the eigensolver as such.

</details>


### [100] [A Vector-Valued Co-Chain/Chain Complex Associated to the Elasticity Complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23829)
*Jennifer Amador Peña,Sara de Ángel,Fernanda Porras*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Christiansen的离散弹性复形，并利用其空间的自由度，构造了一个基于向量值链和上链的新型离散弹性复形，该复形与Christiansen复形同构，且算子虽为代数性质却利用了单纯复形的几何结构。最后定义了从光滑弹性复形到该离散复形的de Rham类映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数值弹性力学中，离散弹性复形为构造稳定的混合有限元方法提供理论基础。研究者希望重新表述Christiansen的离散弹性复形，使其算子更具代数结构且便于计算实现，同时保持与原复形的同构关系。

Method: 本文通过Christiansen复形中各多项式与分布空间的自由度(degrees of freedom)，构造了一个由向量值链(chain)和上链(co-chain)构成的复形。新复形的算子虽然是纯代数的，但其定义依赖于单纯复形的几何信息。此外，作者还构建了一个de Rham型映射，将光滑弹性复形映射到所构造的离散复形。

Result: 成功构造了一个与Christiansen离散弹性复形同构的、基于向量值链和上链的新复形，并建立了从光滑弹性复形到该离散复形的de Rham-like映射。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的视角来理解离散弹性复形，将函数空间层面的弹性复形转化为链与上链层面的代数结构，为弹性问题的有限元离散化提供更清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: In this report, we recall Christiansen's [2011, Num. Math.] discrete elasticity complex that uses spaces of polynomials and distributions. Using the degrees of freedom of those spaces, we write a complex using vector-valued chains and co-chains that is isomorphic to Christiansen's complex. The operators of the new complex are algebraic, but use the geometry of the simplicial complex. Finally, we define a de Rham-like map from the smooth elasticity complex to this discrete complex.

</details>


### [101] [New Efficient Implicit-Explicit Deferred Correction methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23919)
*Lorenzo Micalizzi,Davide Torlo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于常微分方程（ODE）近似的隐式-显式（IMEX）任意高阶延迟校正（DeC）方法，提出了一种基于迭代间插值过程的高效改进，使每次迭代的精度与离散格式的阶数系统匹配，从而在降低计算成本的同时赋予方法自然的p自适应特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的IMEX DeC方法虽然能通过迭代逐阶提升精度，但低阶迭代仍使用高阶离散结构，造成计算浪费；同时缺乏自适应性，难以在实际应用中高效处理不同区域所需的精度需求。

Method: 在连续迭代之间引入插值过程，使每次迭代的精度与所采用的离散化阶数系统匹配。这样低阶迭代在更便宜的低阶离散结构上执行，从而降低计算开销，并赋予方法天然的p自适应特性。方法应用于两个DeC格式家族。

Result: 通过多个数值实验验证了改进方案的有效性，包括刚性ODE和含高阶空间导数的PDE算例，展示了在降低计算成本的同时保持高保真结果的能力，以及自适应策略的有效性，并提供效率评估和稳定性区域图。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进型IMEX DeC方法在计算效率和自适应能力方面具有显著优势，能够在保持高精度的同时降低计算开销，是一类有前景的高阶ODE数值求解方案。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate implicit-explicit (IMEX) arbitrary high-order Deferred Correction (DeC) methods for the approximation of ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Such schemes are characterized by an iterative procedure that increases the order of accuracy by one at each iteration. More precisely, we study an efficient modification based on the introduction of interpolation processes between consecutive iterations, with the aim of systematically matching the accuracy achieved at each iteration with the order of the discretization employed. On the one hand, this modification leads to computational advantages, since the low-order iterations are performed on cheaper lower-order discretization structures; on the other hand, it endows the methods with a natural $p$-adaptive character, which is particularly appealing in the context of practical applications. We investigate this modification for two families of DeC schemes, providing numerical validation, efficiency assessments, and stability region plots. The numerical validation includes several examples involving stiff ODEs and partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-order spatial derivatives. The ability of the modified schemes to provide high-fidelity results at reduced computational cost, as well as the effectiveness of the adaptive strategy, is demonstrated through the numerical experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Structure-preserving generalized transferable neural networks for the Cahn-Hilliard equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23980)
*Cao-Kha Doan,Thi-Thao-Phuong Hoang,Lili Ju,Shuting Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构保持的神经网络框架，用于求解混合形式的Cahn-Hilliard方程。该方法采用广义可迁移神经网络（GTransNet）进行空间逼近，结合稳定的BDF格式进行时间离散，并通过后处理质量守恒投影解决了最小二乘残差导致的质量不守恒问题，实现了时间离散层面上的质量守恒和能量稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard方程描述了相分离过程，其数值求解需要精确保持质量守恒和能量耗散两个物理性质。传统的数值方法在处理复杂域、可变迁移率及长时间模拟时存在局限，而神经网络方法在空间逼近上具有网格无关的优势，但需要确保结构保持性质不被破坏。

Method: 采用GTransNet进行空间逼近，结合一阶和二阶稳定的BDF格式进行时间离散；通过配点法实现，每时间步求解一个常系数矩阵的最小二乘系统以确定输出层权重；针对最小二乘残差导致的质量不守恒问题，引入一种新颖的后处理质量守恒投影，通过极小化问题强制满足质量约束，且计算成本可忽略；隐藏层预先确定，方法无网格化。

Result: 二维和三维的广泛数值实验验证了方法的收敛性、质量守恒和能量耗散性质，同时展示了GTransNet-BDF格式的精度和鲁棒性。方案适用于复杂域、可变迁移率和长时间模拟。

Conclusion: 该GTransNet-BDF框架在保持Cahn-Hilliard方程关键物理结构的同时，兼具神经网络的灵活性和高效的求解效率，为结构保持的神经网络数值方法提供了一个有价值的范例。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a structure-preserving neural network-based framework for the Cahn-Hilliard equation in mixed form. We employ generalized transferable neural networks (GTransNet) for spatial approximation and stabilized backward differentiation formulas (BDF) for temporal discretization. The resulting first- and second-order in time GTransNet-BDF schemes are shown to conserve mass and satisfy energy stability at the time-discrete level.
