### [186] [Weak irreducibility as a spectral criterion for phase coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23757)
*Onofre Rojas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于谱分析的有限尺寸共存的统一理论框架，通过"扇区失衡"和"扇区间连通性"两个谱坐标来区分伪相变与真实热力学相共存，并证明真实相共存是有限尺寸避让共存的奇异极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限尺寸理论通过自由能竞争和界面隧穿描述一级相变共存，但扇区平衡与扇区间连通性的不同谱角色通常未被明确区分，缺乏统一的谱框架来解释伪相变与真实相共存之间的本质联系与区别。

Method: 通过两个谱坐标（扇区失衡定位平衡、连通性决定平衡是否保持避让）来刻画相共存。在平衡点，有限连通性产生具有等谱权重的唯一主导态；对具有正界面张力的短程系统，界面成本指数抑制连通性，关闭共存分裂并渐近恢复可约性。

Result: 真实相共存作为有限尺寸避让共存的奇异极限出现，而一维伪相变保持有限连通性。在装饰双层伊辛模型中验证了共存隙随独立估计的指数闭合行为。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了伪相变与热力学相共存的谱描述，明确了两者由连通性是否被指数抑制所区分的物理本质，为有限尺寸一级相变提供更精确的谱刻画工具。

Abstract: Conventional finite-size theories describe first-order coexistence through free-energy competition and interfacial tunneling, but the distinct spectral roles of sector balance and inter-sector connectivity are not usually explicit. We show that pseudo-transitions and thermodynamic phase coexistence are governed by two spectral coordinates: sector imbalance locates balance, while connectivity determines whether that balance remains avoided. At balance, finite connectivity produces a unique dominant state with equal spectral weights in the symmetrized two-sector representation. For short-range systems with positive interface tension, interfacial costs suppress the connectivity exponentially, close the coexistence splitting, and asymptotically restore reducibility. Thus, genuine phase coexistence emerges as the singular limit of finite-size avoided coexistence, whereas one-dimensional pseudo-transitions retain finite connectivity. In a decorated bilayer Ising model, the coexistence gap at independently est

</details>


### [187] [Entropy Production Bounds the Accuracy of Computation in Markov Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23764)
*Songela W. Chen,David T. Limmer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了可逆连续时间马尔可夫网络中动态计算的普适热力学极限，指出计算误差受熵产生率与记忆时间乘积的下界约束，意味着精确的动态计算需要大量耗散或长寿命的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物和人工网络通过将时间依赖输入转化为功能输出来进行计算。由于随机网络内部状态在有限时间尺度上弛豫，输出会滞后于变化的环境从而产生计算误差。此前缺乏对这类计算误差的普适热力学下界的理解，本文旨在建立这种极限。

Method: 将总误差分解为表示误差（representation error）和滞后误差（lag error）两部分，针对可逆连续时间马尔可夫网络推导出滞后误差与熵产生率及记忆时间（等于输出可观测量平衡自相关的积分）之间的不等式关系。

Result: 推导出的不等式表明，精确的动态计算需要要么有大量耗散（高熵产生率），要么有由缓慢弛豫模式编码的长寿命记忆。该原理在人工马尔可夫网络和生物化学信息处理模型中得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究确立了随机网络中信息处理的热力学极限，为理解生物计算的能量代价提供了定量框架，揭示了计算精度与热力学耗散之间的根本权衡关系。

Abstract: Biological and artificial networks compute by transforming time-dependent inputs into functional outputs. Because the internal state of a stochastic network relaxes on finite timescales, its output generally lags behind a changing environment, producing computational errors. We show that for reversible continuous-time Markov networks the error admits a universal thermodynamic bound. Decomposing the total error into representation and lag contributions, we derive an inequality relating the lag error to the entropy production rate and a memory time equal to the integrated equilibrium autocorrelation of the output observable. The bound implies that accurate dynamical computation requires either substantial dissipation or long-lived memory encoded in slowly relaxing modes. We demonstrate these principles in artificial Markov networks and in models of biochemical information processing. Our results establish a thermodynamic limit on information processing in stochastic networks and provide a quantitative framework for understanding the energetic costs of biological computation.

</details>


### [188] [Jamming states in random sequential adsorption of diffusion-limited aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23905)
*Fahad Puthalath,Dipanjan Mandal,Sumanta Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了方形晶格上扩散限制聚集（DLA）团簇的不可逆吸附（堵塞）行为，通过系统控制团簇形状多样性，发现堵塞密度随团簇尺寸以幂律下降，且形状多分散性促进更紧密的堆积，不同模型变体间的差异随尺寸增大趋于消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界和工程系统中普遍存在分形沉积物，理解DLA团簇的不可逆吸附及其堵塞特性具有重要理论意义。此前研究较少关注团簇形状多样性对堵塞性质的系统影响，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在方形晶格上开展大规模模拟，通过系统控制所用不同形状团簇的数量（覆盖单分散和多分散模型变体），研究团簇尺寸2≤k≤4096范围内的堵塞行为，分析堵塞密度、幂律指数α及波动的标度行为。

Result: 堵塞密度随团簇尺寸以幂律pj(k)-pj^∞∼k^-α下降；α在0.374(2)到0.417(1)之间，两者均依赖形状多样性程度；增大形状多分散性促进更致密堆积；堵塞密度波动对固定形状池按σ(L)∼1/L标度，而对刷新形状池则与L无关；不同模型变体间差异随k增大而减小，预期在k→∞时消失。

Conclusion: DLA团簇的形状多样性对堵塞密度、标度指数和波动行为均有显著影响，但不同模型变体间的差异最终会因DLA团簇的统计自相似性而在大尺寸极限下消失。

Abstract: Motivated by the ubiquity of ramified fractal deposits in nature and engineered systems, we investigate the irreversible adsorption of diffusion-limited aggregation (DLA) clusters on a square lattice. We study the role of cluster shape diversity on jamming properties of the system by systematically controlling the number of distinct shapes used across and within realizations, encompassing both monodisperse and polydisperse model variants. Our large-scale simulations over a broad range of cluster sizes $2\leqslant k\leqslant4096$ show that the jamming density decreases with cluster size as a power-law $p_j(k)-p_j^\infty\sim k^{-α}$. Both $α$ and $p_j^\infty$ are found to depend on the degree of shape diversity, with $α$ ranging from $0.374(2)$ to $0.417(1)$. It is observed that increasing shape polydispersity promotes denser packing. Importantly, the fluctuations of the jamming density exhibit distinct scaling behavior: $σ(L)\sim1/L$ for a fixed pool of cluster shape(s), but remain $L$-independent when the pool of shape(s) is refreshed across different realizations. Furthermore, our results demonstrate that the differences between the model variants systematically diminish with increasing $k$ and are expected to vanish as $k\to\infty$ due to the statistical self-similarity of the DLA clusters.

</details>


### [189] [Bulk Phase Transition and Edge Behavior in Temporally Correlated Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23944)
*Masato Hisakado,Takuya Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由行独立的平稳高斯序列构建的长程相关Wigner型矩阵。对于指数衰减(AR(1))相关性，体谱密度通过显式的组合"枢纽"机制从半圆律变形，同时验证了矩阵Dyson方程(MDE)框架的平坦性和衰减假设，数值证据支持在固定ρ<1下的Tracy-Widom边缘普适性；退化极限ρ→1⁻简化为对称Volterra算子。对于幂律相关性dt~t^{-γ}，识别出γ_c=1/2为体四阶矩发散的临界点，γ=1为MDE边缘分析平坦性条件失效的标志，并证明四阶矩转变是精确的，数值表明自洽边缘在γ=1处平滑变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程相关随机矩阵（尤其是Wigner型矩阵）的谱统计行为，探索相关性衰减速率如何影响谱密度形态、边缘普适性以及MDE框架的适用边界，为相关随机矩阵理论建立更完整的图景。

Method: 采用组合方法识别AR(1)相关性下的"枢纽"机制来推导谱密度变形；利用MDE（矩阵Dyson方程）框架验证理论条件；结合数值模拟验证Tracy-Widom普适性和边缘行为；通过精确计算证明幂律相关性下四阶矩的转变，并在γ=1处数值考察自洽边缘的连续性。

Result: 指数衰减情形下体谱密度远离半圆律但满足MDE框架假设，数值支持Tracy-Widom边缘普适性；退化极限联系到Volterra算子与奇异值级联。幂律情形下精确证明γ_c=1/2处四阶矩发散，γ=1处MDE平坦性失效，但数值显示边缘参数在γ=1处平滑过渡，无扭结或不连续。

Conclusion: 长程相关Wigner矩阵展现了丰富的谱结构：指数衰减下谱密度变形但保留普适性，幂律衰减下存在两个特征尺度（γ_c=1/2控制四阶矩、γ=1控制MDE边缘框架），边缘跨越γ=1仍保持平滑，拓展了随机矩阵理论在非独立、长程相关情形下的适用范围。

Abstract: We study long-range correlated Wigner-type matrices built from row-independent stationary Gaussian sequences. For exponentially decaying (AR(1)) correlations, the bulk spectral density deforms from the semicircle law via an explicit combinatorial "hub" mechanism, yet we verify the flatness and decay hypotheses of the matrix-Dyson-equation framework (MDE), with numerical evidence supporting Tracy-Widom edge universality for every fixed $ρ<1$ of the exponential decay correlations; the degenerate limit $ρ\to1^-$ reduces to a symmetrized Volterra operator, connecting to the singular-value cascade identified in a companion BBP analysis. For power-law correlations $dt\sim t^{-γ}$, we identify $γ_c=1/2$ as the critical point for divergence of the bulk fourth-moment, while $γ=1$ marks the breakdown of the flatness condition governing the MDE edge analysis. We prove the fourth-moment transition exactly and find numerically that the self-consistent edge varies smoothly across $γ=1$, with no evidence of a kink or discontinuity.

</details>


### [190] [Geometric Thermodynamics of Scallop Motion with Two Control Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24158)
*Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明热涨落可以绕过Purcell scallop定理的限制，在粘性流体中通过双参数驱动势能景观实现净推进，并建立了连接流体动力学摩擦、随机力学和热力学权衡的统一几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Purcell的scallop定理指出自由度为1的往复形状变形在粘性流体中无法产生净推进，这一经典限制是否可以在热涨落存在时被突破是一个基础性问题。

Method: 通过Smoluchowski方程描述随机形状动力学（含位置依赖的迁移率M_eff(x)），使用广义逆算子评估慢驱动响应；控制参数的循环调制产生非零的Berry-Sinitsyn-Nemenman曲率F_12，从而驱动定向几何推进；非绝热超额耗散由黎曼热力学度量g_ij描述。

Result: 在双参数驱动势能景观中，热涨落能够绕过scallop定理的限制实现净推进；推进由Berry-Sinitsyn-Nemenman曲率主导，而耗散由黎曼热力学度量刻画，两者共同构成统一的几何框架。

Conclusion: 该研究为微游泳器建立了统一几何基础，将流体动力学摩擦、随机力学与热力学权衡联系起来，表明热涨落是打破经典流体力学对称性限制的关键机制。

Abstract: According to Purcell's scallop theorem, reciprocal single-degree-of-freedom shape deformations cannot achieve net propulsion in a viscous fluid. We show that this limitation is bypassed by thermal fluctuations in a two-parameter driven potential landscape. Formulating the stochastic shape dynamics via a Smoluchowski equation with position-dependent mobility $M_\mathrm{eff}(x)$, we utilize a generalized inverse operator to evaluate the slow-driving response. Cyclic modulation of the control parameters induces a non-zero Berry-Sinitsyn-Nemenman curvature $F_{12}(\bmθ)$, resulting in directed geometric propulsion. Simultaneously, the non-adiabatic excess dissipation is dictated by a Riemannian thermodynamic metric $g_{ij}(\bmθ)$. Our results provide a unified geometric foundation that bridges hydrodynamic friction, stochastic mechanics, and thermodynamic trade-offs in micro-swimmers.

</details>


### [191] [A Minimal Thermodynamically Consistent Chemical Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24200)
*Benedikt Remlein,Luca Monari,Beatrice Bartolomei,Giulio Ragazzon,Massimiliano Esposito,Emanuele Penocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个比化学Hopf分岔最小反应网络及其可逆扩展更为极简的热力学一致反应网络，该网络在三内部物种耦合两个化学恒化物种的约束下，仅含单分子和双分子可逆反应，在恒化器浓度变化时经历超临界Hopf分岔，并通过正规形约化方法解析地刻画了振荡起始附近的热力学量（如半巨吉布斯自由能和非保守做功率）在分岔点的扭结型不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧凑振荡网络难以同时满足化学上合理的单一/双分子反应结构、反应可逆性和解析可解性这三个特性。此前对热力学量在Hopf分岔处的响应（如kink型不连续）仅能通过数值模拟复杂网络来表征，缺乏解析描述，因此需要构造一个极小且热力学一致的网络模型来实现解析分析。

Method: 构造一个由三个内部物种（耦合两个恒化物种）组成的极小可逆反应网络，仅包含单分子和双分子反应；采用正规形约化（normal-form reduction）数学技术，获得保持完整可逆网络主导相空间结构的受控不可逆近似，从而解析地计算分岔结构和热力学可观测量（半巨吉布斯自由能、非保守做功率）的领头阶贡献。

Result: 该系统在改变恒化器浓度时发生超临界Hopf分岔；解析计算表明半巨吉布斯自由能和非保守做功率在Hopf分岔处展现出扭结型（kink-like）不连续性。这一结果将此前仅能通过数值模拟更复杂网络获得的现象提升为解析可描述的形式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个兼具化学可行性、可逆性和解析可解性的极小自振荡热力学模型，并通过此模型解析证实了Hopf分岔处热力学量的奇异响应行为，为理解非平衡驱动下化学振荡系统的热力学特性提供了理论基石。

Abstract: Inspired by the chemical reaction network considered as the smallest system featuring a Hopf bifurcation and its reversible extension, we introduce an even more minimal reaction network that is thermodynamically consistent and exhibits autonomous oscillations under nonequilibrium driving. The model combines three features that are rarely realized simultaneously in compact oscillator networks: a chemically plausible structure restricted to uni- and bimolecular reactions, reversibility of such reactions, and analytical tractability. The system consists of three internal species coupled to two chemostatted species and still undergoes a supercritical Hopf bifurcation when a chemostat concentration is varied. To analyze the dynamics and thermodynamics near the onset of oscillations, we employ the mathematical technique of normal-form reduction, which allows obtaining a controlled irreversible approximation that preserves the leading phase-space structure of the full reversible network while enabling explicit calculations. This framework provides analytical access to the bifurcation structure and the leading contributions governing thermodynamic observables. Within this setting, we use the onset of oscillations to characterize the thermodynamic response of the system. In particular, we offer an analytical description of key thermodynamic quantities such as the semi-grand Gibbs free energy and the non-conservative work rate, which exhibit a kink-like discontinuity at the Hopf bifurcation. We thus provide an analytical description of a phenomenon previously characterized only through numerical simulations of more complex networks.

</details>


### [192] [Exact autoregressive sampling of planar Ising spin glasses via the Kac--Ward theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24382)
*Jing Liu,Tao Chen,Tianrui Che,Lei Wang,Youjin Deng,Pan Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kac-Ward理论的精确自回归采样算法，用于平面Ising自旋玻璃。通过辅助自旋构造将条件配分函数映射到零场Ising模型，利用Kac-Ward行列式公式精确求值，生成严格独立同分布的样本，计算复杂度为O(N^{5/2})，为神经自回归采样器提供精确基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自旋玻璃的Boltzmann分布中精确采样仍然是一个悬而未决的挑战。马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法受困于临界减速和亚稳陷阱，而神经自回归采样器（如变分自回归网络）是近似方法，且在缺乏精确参考样本的情况下无法被严格基准测试。

Method: 基于Kac-Ward理论设计精确自回归采样算法。在链式法则因子分解下，逐自旋固定会诱导边界局域化外场，破坏精确求值所需的零场结构。通过保持平面性的辅助自旋构造编码这些外场，将条件配分函数映射为扩展的零场Ising模型，并用Kac-Ward行列式公式精确计算。

Result: 该方法以O(N^{5/2})的计算复杂度（N为自旋数）生成严格独立同分布且具有精确归一化似然的样本。

Conclusion: 该算法为神经自回归采样器提供了精确的基准参照，解决了自旋玻璃精确采样领域长期存在的难题。

Abstract: Exact sampling from the Boltzmann distribution of spin glasses remains an outstanding challenge: Markov chain Monte Carlo methods suffer from critical slowing down and metastable trapping, while modern neural autoregressive samplers such as variational autoregressive networks are approximate and, in the absence of exact reference samples, cannot be rigorously benchmarked. Here we present an exact autoregressive sampling algorithm for planar Ising spin glasses based on the Kac--Ward theory. Under the chain-rule factorization, sequentially fixing spins induces boundary-localized external fields, which destroy the zero-field structure required for exact evaluation. By encoding these fields with a planarity-preserving auxiliary spin construction, the conditional partition functions are mapped to an extended zero-field Ising model and exactly evaluated using the Kac--Ward determinant formula. The method generates strictly independent and identically distributed samples with exact normalized likelihoods at a computational cost of $\mathcal{O}(N^{5/2})$ for $N$ spins, thereby providing an exact baseline for benchmarking neural autoregressive samplers.

</details>


### [193] [Emergence of Stigmergic Transport in Granular Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24383)
*F. Wéry,F. N. Pinan Basualdo,B. Gorissen,N. Vandewalle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过机器人随机行走实验和最小随机模型，揭示了在可变形环境中，环境记忆与几何拥挤的相互作用能够自发产生stigmergic路径形成，并在低于jamming转变的堆积分数下存在最优输运区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性粒子如何在可变形介质中通过纯机械相互作用集体构建输运网络，探索环境记忆与拥挤效应之间的竞争关系对路径形成的调控机制。

Method: 采用机器人随机行走器实验与最小随机模型相结合的方法，系统改变堆积分数（packing fraction），测量路径形成的临界堆积分数φ_c以及jamming转变φ_J附近的输运增强行为。

Result: 发现当堆积分数超过临界值φ_c时，出现自增强的持续输运路径，环境记忆增强行走器迁移率；但随着接近jamming转变φ_J，拥挤效应逐渐抑制这种输运增强，形成非单调行为，并在远低于jamming处存在最优输运状态。

Conclusion: 该工作证明活性剂可通过与可变形基底的纯机械相互作用集体构建输运网络，为理解stigmergic自组织行为在非化学信号环境中的普适性提供了新视角。

Abstract: We show that stigmergic path formation emerges in a deformable environment through the interplay between environmental memory and geometrical crowding. Using experiments with robotic random walkers together with a minimal stochastic model, we demonstrate the onset of persistent self-reinforced transport pathways above a critical packing fraction $φ_c$, where environmental memory enhances walker mobility. As the jamming transition $φ_J$ is approached, increasing crowding progressively suppresses this transport enhancement despite the persistence of environmental memory. The resulting non-monotonic behavior reveals an optimal transport regime well below jamming. More generally, our work establishes how active agents can collectively build transport networks through purely mechanical interactions with a deformable substrate.

</details>


### [194] [Dynamic quantum phase transitions in the two-leg Creutz ladder with long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24514)
*J. A. da Silva,J. C. Xavier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有长程跃迁（幂律衰减指数ν、有限范围D）的双腿Creutz模型的量子淬灭动力学。通过精确求解一般的双带模型，计算Loschmidt振幅和动力学自由能f(t)，并确定Yang-Lee-Fisher（YLF）零点。研究发现临界时间数量N_s依赖于ν和D，在小ν大D的极限下临界时间趋于稠密，形成非解析时间的稠密集，与Xavier和Hoyos在SSH模型中的发现类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解长程跃迁对量子淬灭后动力学相变（Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions, DQPT）的影响，特别是跃迁强度随距离幂律衰减时，Loschmidt回响的非解析行为如何随跃迁指数ν和范围D变化，以揭示长程相互作用对动力学临界现象的普适性影响。

Method: 1) 首先在动量空间精确求解一般的双带模型，给出Loschmidt振幅的解析表达式；2) 据此计算动力学自由能f(t)；3) 通过求解非线性方程确定YLF零点；4) 证明双腿Creutz模型在动量空间是这一般双带模型的特殊情形；5) 利用上述结果分析动力学自由能的非解析行为，确定临界时间t_c及其数量N_s对ν和D的依赖关系。

Result: 作者发现非平凡临界时间的数目N_s同时依赖于幂律指数ν和跃迁范围D。具体而言，当ν较小且D较大时，临界时间在时间轴上变得越来越稠密，在适当的参数区间内会在稠密的时间集合上出现非解析行为，这与Xavier和Hoyos在具有长程跃迁的SSH模型中的观察结果一致。

Conclusion: 长程跃迁的幂律指数ν和有限范围D共同调控着动力学的非解析行为：在小ν大D时有致密化的临界时间结构，暗示长程跃迁可能使DQPT的非解析时间点集趋于稠密，从而改变量子淬灭动力学的定性图像。这一结论推广了SSH模型中的结果，表明该现象可能具有更广泛的普适性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate quantum quenches in the two-leg Creutz model with long-range hopping, where the hopping amplitudes decay with distance as a power law characterized by an exponent $ν$ and have a finite range $D$. We first obtain the exact solution of a generic two-band model in momentum space. This allows us to compute the Loschmidt amplitude and, consequently, the dynamical free energy $f(\texttt{t})$ of the two-band model. We also show how to determine the Yang-Lee Fisher (YLF) zeros by solving a nonlinear equation. We demonstrate that the two-leg Creutz model in momentum space is a special case of the generic two-band model. Using these results, we identify the non-analyticities in the dynamical free energy $f(\texttt{t})$ at critical times $\texttt{t}_{c}$. We find that the number of nontrivial critical times $N_{s}$ depends on both $ν$ and $D$. In particular, we show that for small $ν$ and large $D$ the critical times become increasingly dense, leading, in the appropriate regime, to non-analyticities at an increasingly dense set of times---similar to what was observed by Xavier and Hoyos [Phys. Rev. B, $\textbf{108}$, 214303 (2023)] in the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model with long-range hopping terms.

</details>


### [195] [Transport interpretation of entanglement Hamiltonian cumulants in integrable quantum quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24613)
*Riccardo Travaglino,Pasquale Calabrese*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究相互作用可积模型中全局量子淬灭后纠缠哈密顿量的累积量动力学，证明这些累积量精确等于所选取宏态中相应守恒荷电流的累积量，从而在纠缠动力学与输运之间建立了直接联系，为冯·诺依曼熵的准粒子图像提供了输运对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的准粒子图像成功地描述了冯·诺依曼熵的演化，但对更高阶的纠缠谱统计（纠缠哈密顿量的累积量）缺乏统一的输运层面理解。作者希望建立纠缠动力学与输运之间的直接联系，并在自由、共形和相互作用可积系统中获得统一描述。

Method: 基于空间-时间对偶（space-time duality）的最新结果，分析相互作用可积模型中全局量子淬灭后纠缠哈密顿量的累积量，将其与合适守恒荷电流的累积量在初始态所选宏态下进行精确对应。在自由费米子和共形极限下简化为左右传播激发电流累积量之差，在相互作用情形下通过纠缠谱层面保留类似结构。

Result: 证明纠缠哈密顿量的累积量精确等于相应守恒荷电流在宏态下的累积量。自由费米子与共形极限还原为已知的左右传播激发电流累积量之差；在相互作用可积模型中，尽管算符层面无法分解为独立左右传播子，类似的分解结构在纠缠谱层面仍然成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了纠缠动力学与输运之间的一般性对应关系，为纠缠哈密顿量涨落提供了跨越自由、共形和相互作用可积系统的统一描述框架。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of the cumulants of the entanglement Hamiltonian in interacting integrable models following global quantum quenches. Building on recent results based on space-time duality, we show that these cumulants are exactly given by the cumulants of currents of suitable conserved charges evaluated in the macrostate selected by the initial state. This establishes a direct connection between entanglement dynamics and transport, providing a transport counterpart to the quasiparticle picture that successfully describes the evolution of the von Neumann entropy. In the free-fermion and conformal limits, our results reduce to the difference between the current cumulants carried by right- and left-moving excitations, recovering previously known expressions. In interacting integrable models, where a decomposition into independent right and left movers is no longer meaningful at the operator level, a similar structure survives at the level of the entanglement spectrum, yielding a unified description of entanglement Hamiltonian fluctuations across free, conformal, and interacting integrable systems.

</details>


### [196] [Ideal Bose-Einstein condensation in the canonical ensemble: exact asymptotic estimates from large deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24625)
*Giacomo Gradenigo,Dario Lucente,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大偏差理论计算自由玻色子正则配分函数的方法，在固定密度系综中研究三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚，通过无量纲参数ℓ=L/λ_T提供临界密度上下两侧配分函数的精确渐近估计，并给出凝聚分数及其涨落的解析表达式，揭示了BEC的混合阶相变本质及其与DNLS方程中局域化转变的相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算自由玻色子系统的正则配分函数在临界点附近具有挑战性，尤其需要考虑有限尺寸效应。传统方法难以显式描述凝聚转变的驱动机理及其混合阶特征，因此需要一种能够在所有物理区域提供解析结果的统一渐近框架。

Method: 采用大偏差理论方法，以无量纲参数ℓ=L/λ_T作为大参数，对固定密度系综中的三维自由玻色子系统进行分析。通过构造配分函数的大偏差速率函数，在临界密度ϱ_c上下两侧分别给出配分函数的精确渐近估计，并由此推导凝聚分数⟨n_0(ϱ)⟩及其涨落σ_0(ϱ)的解析表达式。

Result: 在所有物理区域（临界密度上下）均获得了配分函数、凝聚分数及其涨落的前导阶解析表达式。成功重现了凝聚区域（ϱ>ϱ_c）中涨落的反常标度σ_0(ϱ)~1/V^{1/3}。该方法完整捕获了相变的一阶方面，并揭示了其驱动机理：凝聚相与正常相的竞争概率。

Conclusion: 大偏差方法为理解玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚提供了一种统一的解析框架，能够显式处理有限尺寸效应，并阐明了BEC的混合阶相变本质，进而揭示了其与DNLS方程中局域化转变等混合阶相变之间的相似性。

Abstract: In this work we present a large-deviations approach to the calculation of the canonical partition function for free bosons. Three-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensation is studied in the fixed-density ensemble as a function of the dimensionless density $\varrho = ρλ_T^3$, with $ρ=N/L^3$ the standard particle density, $λ_T$ the thermal wavelength, $L$ the linear size of the box and $N$ the total number of particles. A large-deviations approach in terms of the dimensionless parameter $\ell=L/λ_T$ allows us to provide exact asymptotic estimates of the canonical partition function both above and below the critical density $\varrho_c$ for Bose-Einstein condensation. We show how this approach allows to explicitly account for finite-size effects and how it fully captures the first-order aspects of the transition, allowing us to explicitate its driving mechanism in terms of the competing probabilities of normal and condensed phases. The proposed large-deviations approach allows then to obtain in all regimes explicit and simple analytical expressions, at the leading order in the large parameter $\ell$, for both the average fraction of particles in the ground state, the condensate fraction $\langle n_0(\varrho) \rangle = \langle N_0(\varrho) \rangle/N$, and for its fluctuations, $σ_0(\varrho) = \sqrt{\langle N_0^2(\varrho)\rangle - \langle N_0(\varrho)\rangle^2}/N$, retrieving for instance the anomalous scaling $σ_0(\varrho)\sim 1/V^{1/3}$ in the condensed regime, $\varrho > \varrho_c$. Our large-deviations asymptotic estimate, by analytically clarifying the mixed-order nature of Bose-Einstein condensation, allows then to reveal the similarity between this transition and other mixed-order transitions, as for instance the localization transition in the Discrete Non-Linear Schrödinger Equation.

</details>


### [197] [Kinetic Turnover in the Early-Stage Nucleation of Multi-Shell Condensed Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24792)
*Kaicheng Zhu,Haibin Su*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个多壳层模型，将结构重排与凝聚簇早期生长耦合，以解决实验测量的成核速率与理论预测之间的数量级差距。研究发现重排速率改变可导致成核速率变化数个数量级，且耦合强度增加时，平均首达时间与重排响应性之间从单调关系转变为非单调的翻转效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测量的成核速率与理论预测之间存在显著偏差，特别是在多步路径中发生动态结构重排时。这一差距阻碍了对相变和相分离中成核这一限速过程的理解，亟需一个能捕捉结构重排与成核耦合机制的框架。

Method: 开发了一个具有时空依赖序参量场的多壳层模型来描述重排-成核过程，其中结构重组与凝聚簇的早期生长相耦合。通过随机模拟跟踪生长簇内部异质结构序的时域演化，并对早期成核阶段的首达问题进行路径分析；引入有效成核势垒来量化非平衡性质。

Result: 改变重排速率可使成核速率改变数个数量级；耦合强度增加时，平均首达时间与重排响应性之间的关系从单调变为非单调，呈现翻转效应；这些行为可通过考虑非平衡性质的有效成核势垒进行定量合理化。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了多步成核背后的机制，为未来研究提供了一个预测性框架，弥合了实验测量与理论预测之间的差距。

Abstract: Nucleation is a key rate-limiting process in phase transition and phase separation. Recent studies highlight a significant discrepancy between experimentally measured nucleation rates and theoretical predictions, particularly when dynamic structural reordering occurs along multi-step pathways. To bridge this gap, we develop a multi-shell model with a space-time-dependent order-parameter field to describe the reordering-nucleation process, where structural reorganization couples with the early growth of condensed clusters. Through stochastic simulations, we track the time-resolved evolution of heterogeneous structural order inside growing clusters. Path analysis of the first-passage problem in early-stage nucleation demonstrates that shifting the reordering rate alters the nucleation rate by several orders of magnitude. Furthermore, as the coupling strength increases, the relationship between the mean first-passage time and reordering susceptibility shifts from monotonic to non-monotonic, exhibiting a turnover effect. We quantitatively rationalize these behaviors with an effective nucleation barrier that accounts for non-equilibrium properties. Our findings elucidate the mechanisms behind multi-step nucleation and offer a predictive framework for future studies.

</details>


### [198] [Fast generation of spectrally-shaped disorder, on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24867)
*Mathias Casiulis,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出FaSHIoNPOp算法，利用快速非均匀球谐变换，在球面上以O(N log N)复杂度对无序点图案施加傅里叶空间性质约束（如对关联），首次将此类优化方法从平面欧几里得空间扩展到弯曲曲面，可生成球面超均匀结构、陀螺形态结构，并可与全局或局部约束结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的通过傅里叶空间损失函数优化无序点图案的方法严格局限于平坦欧几里得空间，无法用于曲面光子涂层设计或弯曲流形采样等场景，因此需要将这一成功范式推广到球面等弯曲几何上。

Method: 引入FaSHIoNPOp算法，基于快速非均匀球谐变换（O(N log N)复杂度）在球面上强制执行点图案的对关联（pair correlations）性质，并可与全局约束（如中心对称）及局部实空间约束（如对排斥）联合使用。

Result: 算法在球面上成功生成超均匀结构用于采样和物理应用，并设计了陀螺形态（在给定频率具有最大散射功率的无序结构）的球面版本；还展示了结合全局中心对称约束可设计更各向同性的三维陀螺形态。

Conclusion: 该工作为弯曲流形上的最优采样和涂层设计开辟了新途径，对物理、材料和计算机科学多个领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: The design of disordered point patterns with desirable properties is an exciting and ongoing research endeavor, with applications ranging from materials to computer science. A successful approach in recent years has been the optimization of point patterns through a loss function that enforces properties in their Fourier-space representation. Yet, these methods have so far strictly been limited to flat Euclidean space, precluding their use in the contexts of coatings of curved surfaces for photonics, or of sampling of curved manifolds for instance. We introduce FaSHIoNPOp, an algorithm that relies on fast non-uniform spherical harmonics transforms, to enforce pair correlations in point patterns on the sphere with an $O(N \log N)$ complexity in $N$ the number of points. Having demonstrated its performance, we showcase applications of FaSHIoNPOp, ranging from the generation of hyperuniform structures on the sphere for sampling and physical applications, to the design of gyromorphs (disordered structures with maximal scattering power at a given frequency) on the sphere. We additionally show that FaSHIoNPOp can be combined to both global constraints like centrosymmetry, with applications to the design of more isotropic $3d$ gyromorphs, and local real-space constraints like pair repulsion. Our work paves the way for optimal sampling and coating design on curved manifolds, with many applications across physics, materials and computer science.

</details>
